本次報告：法說會

儒鴻 (1476 TT) 
不利因素消退，營運展望正向

永豐觀點 
隨著美國期中選舉結果出爐，關稅及戰爭兩大不利因素有望逐步消退，有利成衣產
業評價回升。公司訂單能見度維持六個月，新興品牌客戶訂單動能強勁，推升營收
及獲利表現，後市展望持續正向，研究部上調儒鴻目標價，維持買進投資評等。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議維持買進，目標價上調至 500 元：儒鴻 3Q26 毛利率表現優於預期，反
映新興品牌客戶訂單動能強勁，改善產品組合，且原材料成本壓力舒緩，帶動獲利
表現回升。展望後市，儒鴻訂單能見度維持六個月，新興品牌客戶訂單動能持續成
長強勁，既有品牌客戶亦維持穩定回補庫存力道，營運展望正向。研究部預估公司
2026/2027 稅後 EPS 25.39/27.79 元，上調本益比評價至 18X，依 2027 之 EPS
進行評價，目標價上調至 500 元，投資評等維持買進，依公司去年股利配發率
74.63%估算，以昨日收盤價來看，目前約具備 4.9%殖利率，應具備下檔殖利率保
護。

ESG 評析 
儒鴻企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B 等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

### Page 2

2

儒鴻為國內成衣業龍頭廠商：儒鴻成立於 1977 年，主要生產彈性針織布與運動服
飾，並垂直整合染整、織布到成衣製造，具有一條龍整合優勢，此外公司持續提升
布料研發和服飾設計能力，積極研發出具流行性、運動性、休閒性、環保性等功能
性之布料，廣泛應用於高級時裝、運動服、休閒服裝、內睡衣、韻律裝等多樣化產
品系列，並銷售至全球知名品牌及零售商，公司 2025 年營收結構為：成衣 66.4%、
針織布 33.6%，銷售地區以美洲為主，主要客戶包括 Nike、Lululemon、Under 
Armour 等知名大廠。 
3Q26 EPS 6.36 元，符合預期：儒鴻 3Q26 營收 105.72 億元（+3.9% QoQ，
+8.8% YoY），略優於預期，主因新興品牌客戶訂單需求強勁，推升營收表現，毛
利率 31.18%（+1.17pts. QoQ，+2.52pts. YoY），優於預期，除因新興品牌客戶
訂單增長，帶動產品組合改善外，儒鴻持續與客戶進行議價，以轉嫁原材料成本上
漲，推升毛利率表現回升，稅後淨利 17.4 億元（+14.2% QoQ，+7.3% YoY），符
合預期，其中，營業費用高於預期，主因儒鴻獲利強勁，認列季獎金費用較預期高
所致，3Q26 稅後 EPS 6.36 元，符合預期。

### Page 3

3

10 月營收符合預期，4Q26 營收預估季增：儒鴻 10 月營收 34 億元，-3.1% MoM，
+10.7% YoY，符合預期。展望 4Q26，新興品牌客戶訂單動能強勁，將抵銷布料
淡季造成的營收下滑力道，預期 4Q26 營收將季增，且因產品組合持續優化，及原
材料成本陸續轉嫁，毛利率表現有望穩定回升，推升獲利表現，基此，研究部預估
4Q26 營收 106.2 億元（+0.5％ QoQ，+14.6％ YoY），毛利率 31.5%（+0.32 
pts. QoQ，+1.31 pts. YoY），稅後淨利 18.3 億元（+5.2% QoQ，+15.9％ YoY），
稅後 EPS 6.68 元。 
不利因素消退，產業評價回升，維持買進投資建議：展望後市，儒鴻訂單能見度維
持約六個月，其中，新興品牌客戶訂單動能持續成長強勁，既有品牌客戶亦維持穩
定回補庫存力道。隨著美國期中選舉結果出爐，關稅政策、美伊戰爭此些不利公司
營運及評價的兩大變數皆有望消退，儘管公司維持先前營收及毛利率指引，但隨著
大環境不確定性因素下降、產品組合持續轉佳、原物料成本下跌及向下轉嫁、垂直
整合比重提升，應有利營收及毛利率表現穩步走升，推升獲利表現。公司 2027 年
預計將藉由產能去瓶頸，提升約個位數水準，印尼新廠預期將於 4Q27-1Q28 投
表三：季度成衣營收趨勢

### Page 4

4

產，規劃月產能為 180 萬件，將分兩期完成。綜合來看，研究部評估儒鴻訂單動
能維持穩健，隨著公司持續導入新產品及新客人加入，有利儒鴻營運表現提升，而
隨著原材料成本成功轉嫁及產品組合持續好轉，毛利率預期將穩步回升，研究部維
持正向看待儒鴻後市獲利表現。研究部預估 2026 年營收 409 億元（+7.7％YoY），
毛利率 31.21%，稅後淨利 69.7 億元（+26.3％YoY），稅後 EPS 25.39 元，預估
2027 年營收 442.3 億元（+8.2％YoY），毛利率 31.5%，稅後淨利 76.3 億元（+9.5
％YoY），稅後 EPS 27.79 元。隨著大環境不利因素消退，成衣產業評價應回升，
儒鴻過往本益比區間多位於 12-30 倍，研究部上調儒鴻本益比評價至 18X，依 2027
之 EPS 進行評價，目標價上調至 500 元，投資評等維持買進，依公司去年股利配
發率 74.63%估算，以昨日收盤價來看，目前約具備 4.9%殖利率，應具備下檔殖
利率保護。

### Page 2

足元の経済・金融状況と注目点
1
（出所） みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
2026. 11.17
米国では物価高で実質賃金が伸び悩む一方、
カード延滞率に改善の兆し。NY連銀によれば、
延滞率の高止まりはコロナ期の与信拡大が影響
しており、今後も緩やかな改善が続くとの見立て
米家計のクレジットカード延滞率に改善の兆し
米国
10月の消費者物価は市場予想を上回る結果。生産
者物価も高止まり。市場は12月利上げを織り込み。
一方で、金利上昇を背景に中古住宅販売は大幅減。
根強いインフレ圧力と住宅市場の弱さが鮮明に
欧州
ユーロ圏の7～9月期GDPは前期から減速。ドイツ
は小幅プラスも、フランスはマイナス、スペインは堅
調と主要国でばらつき。ユーロ圏全体では雇用の伸
びも横ばいとなっており、景気回復力の鈍さを確認
中国
10月の輸出は高い伸びを維持。半導体や自動車な
どがけん引。一方で輸入も大幅増。生産は輸出堅調
で底堅く推移も、投資は下落幅が拡大し、消費も力
強さ欠き低迷。消費者物価も前月から伸びが鈍化

日本
9月の実質消費支出は10カ月連続のマイナス。景気
ウォッチャー調査も6カ月ぶりに悪化し、家計部門の
弱さを示唆する内容。国内企業物価は原油高などを
受けて再び加速。今後消費者物価へ波及する公算
注目点①
日本の消費は所得の伸びを下回る緩慢な回復。
一方、大型M&Aなどから企業の資金需要は旺盛
で、銀行貸出は高い伸びを維持。預金獲得競争
が激化するなか、安定的な預金調達が重要に
日本の家計は消費に慎重、企業は借入に積極的
注目点②

 当時発行されたカードがその後の延滞率上昇に大きく寄与
― ただし、コロナ期の与信拡大による影響は徐々にはく落。加えて、2023年以降は新規発行時の信用力が改善しており、
雇用・所得環境が大きく悪化しなければ、今後も延滞率の緩やかな改善が続く公算

日本：個人消費は所得の伸びを下回る緩慢な拡大。先行き不安が回復の重石に
5
 個人消費は拡大基調を維持も、所得の伸びを下回る緩慢な拡大
―9月の消費活動指数は前年比＋0.8％と拡大基調を維持したものの、耐久財特需のはく落などから増勢は鈍化
― 実質雇用者報酬が着実に増加する一方、消費の伸びは所得を下回る状況が継続
 先行き不安も家計の慎重な支出姿勢に影響
― 消費者マインドは食品やエネルギーなど身近な消費財の価格上昇に対する懸念から低調に推移
― 中東情勢を巡る不透明感もあり、当面の消費は緩慢な拡大にとどまる見通し

― 中間選挙では、DC規制の強化を掲げる民主党知事候補が各州で勝利。とりわけ、DCの集積が進むテキサス・オハイ
オ・ジョージアでは、長年共和党が知事職を握ってきたものの、民主党候補が勝利する結果。今後、DC開発を巡る規制
が強化され、家計への費用転嫁が抑えられれば、中長期的な電力価格の上昇ペースは緩和される可能性

14.7％まで上昇していることから、供給ショックを受けて輸出価格への転嫁が進んでいる様子がうかがえる

 10月の輸入は同＋41.6％と、前月（＋31.0％）から加速。AI関連製品の価格高騰が全体を押し上げ

日本：7～9月期実質GDPは横ばい圏の推移。消費低調の一方、設備投資が堅調に回復

― 個人消費が2四半期連続でマイナスとなったほか、輸出が一時的要因もあり小幅減となり、成長率を下押し。一方、設備
投資はAI関連需要の堅調さを背景にプラス転換し、成長率を押し上げ
―4～6月期（前期比＋0.4％）から減速し、経済の緩やかな拡大傾向が一服
 今後、2026年度後半の実質GDPは、均してみれば小幅なプラス成長と予想
― 個人消費が精彩を欠く一方で、設備投資の拡大が支えになる構図が継続。2026年度全体では＋1％弱の成長率に

### Page 2

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引
用相關內容。

1

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
內需與房市提供支撐 美國財政及通膨風險升高 
經濟 
⚫ 9 月美國零售與餐飲服務銷售額為 7,450 億美元，月增 0.8%、年增 6.3%；
扣除汽車及零件銷售後月增 0.9%，進一步扣除汽車、零件及加油站後
之核心零售銷售月增 0.2%。加油站、餐飲及非實體零售銷售持續成長，
顯示美國消費需求仍具韌性，惟成長動能較前月略有放緩。 
⚫ 2026 財年美國聯邦政府財政赤字達 1 兆 9,924.7 億美元，年增 12.2%，
主因政府支出成長高於收入。9 月單月由去年同期盈餘轉為 269 億美元
赤字，關稅退稅增加壓縮收入，而全年淨利息支出突破 1 兆美元、年增
8.4%。在聯邦債務總額逾 40 兆美元下，財政赤字擴大及債務融資成本
攀升，持續考驗財政永續性。 
⚫ 9 月美國成屋銷售年率 399 萬戶，月增 1.5%、年減 2.2%；成屋中位數
售價 41.75 萬美元，年增 1.3%，連續 39 個月上漲。待售庫存增至 160
萬戶、供給量 4.8 個月，顯示供給改善，惟高利率環境仍抑制購屋需求。 
⚫ 9 月美國消費者物價指數(CPI)經季節調整後月增 0.6%，高於 8 月之
0.4%，年增率由 3.4%升至 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%，年增率由 2.4%
升至 2.6%。受能源價格上揚影響，能源指數月增 5.2%，其中汽油價格
大幅上漲，成為推升通膨之主要因素；住房指數續增 0.4%。整體而言，
美國通膨壓力較前月升溫，核心物價漲勢亦略有增強。 
⚫ 9 月美國生產者物價指數(PPI)月增 1.3%，年增率由 5.6%升至 6.3%；能
源價格大漲 10.8%為主要推升因素，帶動商品價格月增 2.6%。服務價格
亦月增 0.8%，核心 PPI 年增率由 4.8%升至 5.0%，顯示生產端通膨壓力
持續升高。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 10 月中旬，多位聯準會官員表示通膨仍具壓力。Mary Daly 認為未來可
能仍需升息，但 10 月底會議無迫切行動必要；Beth Hammack 警告若通
膨預期惡化，可能加大緊縮力道；主席 Kevin Warsh 則指出美國經濟仍
具韌性，但能源價格上漲及 AI 投資擴張恐推升通膨壓力。整體而言，
聯準會立場偏鷹，但短期升息時點仍視通膨走勢而定。 
⚫ 美墨關稅談判原接近達成初步協議，惟美國商務部長反對調降汽車及鋼
鋁關稅，使協商進展受阻。墨西哥則爭取降低汽車關稅及擴大零組件抵

### Page 3

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引
用相關內容。

2

減範圍。雙方雖持續磋商，但分歧未解，恐影響北美供應鏈及 USMCA
後續談判。 
短評 
⚫ 9 月美國零售銷售持續成長，顯示民間消費仍具韌性，房市交易亦略有
回溫，反映整體經濟動能尚未明顯轉弱。然而，CPI 與 PPI 漲幅同步擴
大，能源及住房成本壓力持續推升通膨，加上聯邦財政赤字進一步擴大、
全年淨利息支出突破 1 兆美元，凸顯政府財政負擔日益沉重。整體而
言，美國經濟在消費需求支撐下仍維持擴張，但通膨壓力升溫與財政失
衡問題並存，使後續經濟前景面臨更多不確定性。 
歐 
元 
區 
工業生產走疲，前景有待觀察 
經濟 ⚫ 歐元區 8 月工業生產(不含營造業)較 7 月下滑 0.5%，呈現連續三個月下 降。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 歐盟領袖峰會歐盟 27 個成員國普遍支持建立貿易的反制機制。該機制
將賦予歐盟在極端情況下，迅速且全面封鎖特定國家企業進入歐洲單一
市場的權限。 
⚫ 歐中貿易談判公布相關細節，未來中國出口至歐盟的混合動力汽車設定
每年 35 萬輛的配額上限(每年可遞增 6%)。 
短評 
⚫ 雖德國財政擴張有助支持工業生產，但法國政治、選舉等不利前景重挫
市場信心，恐會影響企業以及政府在各項支出、投資決策，進而形成拖
累因素。 
⚫ 歐盟的「下一代歐盟復甦計畫」(NextGenerationEU)將在年底前完成所有
撥款，後續須關注是否有其他替代財政工具來緩解財政真空(近期歐盟
領袖峰會雖有協商相關議題但無法取得共識)，以繼續支撐景氣動能。 
日 
本 
AI 投資續強，帶動機械訂單穩健復甦 
經濟 ⚫ 8 月核心機械訂單月增 5.2%。 
短評

一、 美國—內需與房市提供支撐 美國財政及通膨風險升高 
2026 年 9 月美國零售與餐飲服務銷售額經季節性調整後為 7,450 億美元，較
上月增加 0.8%，年增 6.3%。若扣除汽車及零件銷售，零售額月增 0.9%；進一步
扣除汽車、零件及加油站後，核心零售銷售月增 0.2%，顯示整體消費需求仍維持
成長。從主要業別表現觀察，加油站、非實體零售商及餐飲服務與飲酒場所銷售
額均較上月增加，其中加油站及餐飲消費表現相對亮眼，電子及家電商店銷售亦
呈成長。整體而言，9 月美國零售銷售延續擴張走勢，顯示民間消費仍具支撐力
道，且多數主要消費類別維持正成長，惟核心零售增幅較前月放緩，後續仍須關
注物價、就業及金融情勢變化對消費支出的影響。 
2026 年 9 月(2026 財年第 12 個月)美國聯邦政府財政赤字為 269.0 億美元，較
去年同期 1,979.4 億美元財政盈餘轉為赤字。當月政府收入 5,578.8 億美元，年增
2.6%；政府支出 5,847.8 億美元，年增 69.1%，支出增幅明顯高於收入。收入方
面，9 月關稅淨收入由 8 月的 128.4 億美元降至 59.1 億美元，累計 2026 財年關稅
淨收入約 1,732.2 億美元，年減 11.1%，關稅退稅增加已部分抵銷關稅收入成長。
支出方面，9 月聯邦政府淨利息支出約 347.9 億美元，全年累計達 1 兆 517.5 億美
元，年增 8.4%，反映高利率環境下債務利息負擔持續攀升。累計 2026 財年全年，
美國聯邦政府財政赤字約 1 兆 9,924.7 億美元，較 2025 財年擴大 12.2%。其中，
政府收入年增 3.2%至 5 兆 4,033.3 億美元，支出年增 5.5%至 7 兆 3,958.0 億美元，
顯示支出成長仍快於收入增加。從支出結構觀察，社會安全、醫療保險、國防及
淨利息支出均較上年增加，持續推升財政壓力。整體而言，美國財政赤字擴大主
要反映政府支出成長強勁、利息負擔居高不下，以及關稅退稅增加削弱部分收入
效果。在聯邦債務總額突破 40 兆美元背景下，未來財政赤字走勢、債務融資成本
及財政永續性問題，仍將是市場關注的重要焦點。 
2026 年 9 月美國成屋銷售經季節調整後年率升至 399 萬戶，較上月增加 1.5%，
惟較去年同期減少 2.2%，整體房市成交動能仍偏疲弱。區域表現方面，南部及西
部分別月增 2.8%，中西部持平，東北部月減 2.1%，四大地區銷售量均較去年同
期下滑。房價方面，成屋售價中位數為 41.75 萬美元，年增 1.3%，已連續 39 個

機票、休閒娛樂及服飾等價格上漲，通訊價格則下跌。整體而言，9 月美國通膨
明顯回升，主要受汽油價格大幅上漲及住房成本續增帶動；核心 CPI 月增率維持
0.3%，年增率則回升至 2.6%，顯示核心物價壓力較前月略有增強，未來通膨走勢
仍須持續關注能源價格變化及住房等服務項目價格表現。 
 2026 年 9 月美國生產者物價指數(PPI)經季節調整後月增 1.3%，高於上月之
0.5%，年增率則由 5.6%升至 6.3%。其中，最終需求商品價格月增 2.6%，主要受
能源價格月增 10.8%帶動，以汽油、柴油及航空燃油價格漲幅較為顯著；食品價
格亦月增 1.2%。另一方面，最終需求服務價格月增 0.8%，主要反映批發及零售
利潤率、運輸服務價格上升。剔除食品、能源及貿易服務後之核心最終需求 PPI
月增 0.5%，高於上月之 0.3%，年增率由 4.8%升至 5.0%。整體而言，9 月 PPI 升
幅明顯擴大，主要反映能源與服務價格同步上揚，顯示生產端成本壓力仍偏高，
後續仍須關注能源價格及運輸成本變化對通膨走勢之影響。

先觀察後續數據表現。克里夫蘭聯準銀行總裁哈瑪克(Beth Hammack)則警告，若
通膨預期持續惡化，聯準會可能必須採取更積極的緊縮措施，以維持物價穩定。

近期美墨貿易談判再度受到關稅爭議影響。據《華爾街日報》報導，雙方原

加協定》（USMCA）檢討與延長協商創造有利條件。然而，美國商務部長 Howard 
Lutnick 反對大幅調降目前對墨西哥汽車課徵的 25%關稅，以及鋼鐵、鋁製品與部
分衍生製成品的 50%關稅，使談判進展受阻。墨西哥方面則希望將汽車關稅降至
15%或以下，以縮小與日本、南韓汽車適用稅率的差距，並擴大汽車關稅抵減機
制對美墨加三國零組件價值的認列範圍；惟美方仍須考量國內金屬產業對降稅可
能衝擊本土生產及就業的疑慮。儘管雙方對關稅調整幅度仍存分歧，墨西哥經貿
官員表示協商已有部分進展，美國商務部亦強調持續與墨方保持溝通，顯示談判
尚未正式破局。整體而言，美墨關稅協商結果不僅攸關北美汽車及金屬產業供應
鏈成本與競爭力，也可能影響 USMCA 後續檢討與延長協商進程；若雙方遲遲無
法縮小分歧，恐增加企業投資與供應鏈布局的不確定性，並對北美區域貿易整合
形成壓力。 
二、歐元區—工業生產走疲，前景有待觀察 
 歐元區 8 月工業生產(不含營造業)較 7 月下滑 0.5%，呈現連續三個月下降，
其中非耐久消費財、中間財及能源產出分別減少 2.7%、0.8%、0.6%，僅資本財、
耐久消費財產出分別成長 0.4%、0.2%。儘管德國 8 月工業生產(不含營造業)反彈
0.4%，係受惠車廠陸續結束歲修，但法、義、西工業生產分別下跌 0.3%、1.3%、
0.8%為主要拖累因素，反映先前美伊戰爭帶來的庫存儲備需求逐漸消退，尤其以
能源密集產業(化工、造紙、鋼鐵等)變化最為明顯。整體而言，即便排除能源密集
產業的波動，其他製造業諸如製藥、汽車、機械等生產情形皆偏疲軟。

四、中國—AI 相關需求帶動出口動能強勁成長 
9 月出口年增率較 8 月大幅增加 11.3 個百分點至 36.3%，出口總值為 4,472
億美元；累計前九個月出口年增率上升為 21.3%，累積出口總值為 33,707 億美元。
前九個月出口主要成長項目(累積年增率)包含 IC(108.1%)、自動數據處理設備(包
含伺服器等，54.6%)、船舶(29.9%)、汽車(54.3%)，其中 IC 及自動數據處理設備
出口加速成長，為 9 月出口年增率大幅上升的主要因素。 
9 月進口年增率較 8 月增 2.8 個百分點至 31%，進口總值為 3,137 億美元；累
計前九個月進口值成長 27.5%至 24,326 億美元。其中 IC、自動數據處理設備(包
含伺服器等)進口分別成長 63.5%、124.8%，呼應中國對高階 IC 的依賴，以及部
分伺服器關鍵組件有賴於進口滿足所需。進出口相抵後，前九個月貿易順差達
9,381 億美元，僅較去年同期增加 7.7%，增幅明顯低於出口，顯示出口擴張同時
伴隨關鍵原物料進口大幅增加，稀釋出口成長的加值效果，亦反映部分高附加價
值產品依賴國際供應鏈支持。 
9 月生產者通膨(出廠價格)由 8 月的 3.8%升至 5%，主因原油價格自 7 月起走
升，其影響逐漸反映於上游生產成本。當月石油和天然氣開採業出廠價格年增率
續揚 14.1 個百分點至 24.5%，並帶動燃料加工業出廠價格年增率上揚至 21.6%，
此外，對於原油價格波動較為敏感的化學品、化纖出廠價格年增率加速升至
12.1%、12.3%，顯示原油價格上漲的影響開始擴散至中下游產業。此外，由於近
期鋼鐵價格走弱，黑色金屬礦產開採、冶煉加工業出廠價格分別年減 3.4%、0.2%，

9 月消費者通膨(CPI)升 0.1 個百分點為 0.9%，但仍然是今年以來第三低的水
準，核心通膨(不含食品及能源)持平於 1 %不變。該月燃油通膨續增 6.6 個百分點
至 14.9%，在客運成本增加之下，旅遊服務通膨亦增加 0.8 個百分點至 3.5%，此
外，由於手機價格上漲，通信工具通膨上揚 3.7 個百分點至 14.3%；其他商品及
服務價格則大致平穩。整體而言，雖然企業生產成本持續上升，但在民間消費乏
力之下，企業仍大多自行吸收成本，加上政府壓抑零售燃油及民生水電，故目前
原油價格高漲的影響尚未反映於消費端價格。惟考量能源成本傳導存在時間差，
若油價維持高檔或進一步上漲，未來通膨仍有進一步上升的風險。 
展望未來，歐中談判已就汽車貿易達成部分共識，包含未來中國出口至歐盟
的混合動力汽車設定每年 35 萬輛的配額上限，且配額規模可逐年增加 6%。依照
歐盟預估 2026 年自中國進口約 80 萬輛混合動力汽車推算，中國對歐盟的混合動
力車出口量將較原先潛在規模減少一半以上。雖該協議不利於汽車出口，但有助
於避免歐盟進一步擴大貿易保護措施，中國車廠則可能會加速透過赴歐投資設
廠、提升當地生產比重等方式因應配額限制，藉此維持在歐洲市場的競爭力與市
占率。 
五、印度及東南亞—IMF 上調印度及東協的經濟展望；新、馬第三季 GDP 均逾
5% 
(1)據 IMF2026 年 10 月經濟展望報告(WEO)(詳下表)，印度仍是全球主要經濟體
中成長最快者之一，雖然面對油價上漲及全球不確定性，但內需及民間消費韌性
強勁，服務業持續擴張，2026、2027 年 GDP 成長率預估皆為 7.0%。東協國家方
面，受惠於 AI 伺服器與資料中心投資、科技產業供應鏈重組、美中貿易重組帶
來轉單效應等利多，越南、馬來西亞、新加坡、菲律賓及泰國均為 AI 供應鏈受益
國，但須留意地緣政治、全球貿易重組及 AI 投資循環變化，帶來的通膨及經濟
下行風險，建議各國政府提升農業生產效率及糧食安全，提升數位經濟及服務業
競爭力，強化財政紀律並提高公共投資效率，維持穩健貨幣政策與匯率穩定。

反映地緣政治衝擊與惡劣氣候的影響，食品價格較去年同期上漲 7.74%，同時，
油價上漲和盧比貶值推動交通運輸通膨率升至 5.01%，餐飲和住宿（9.36%）以及
個人照護和社會安全（13.75%）的價格漲幅亦有所上升。9 月批發價格(WPI)大增
11.39%，亦受燃料(25.85%)、製造業(9.76%)和食品(8.66%)價格加速上漲所推動。
由於通膨持續上揚至印度央行 6%的目標上限，恐加大後續升息壓力。 
(3)新加坡：2026 年第三季經濟成長(GDP)季增 1.1%、年增 5.4%，雖較上季增速
放緩，仍為近年較高的成長增速。外需仍為主要成長引擎，全球 AI 相關設備需
求持續擴張，帶動電子產品與機械設備出口成長，加上航空與航運活動活絡，使
貨物及服務出口表現亮眼；相較之下，內需成長較為溫和，零售消費及部分民生
服務需求仍相對疲弱。從產業面分析，製造業年增 7.5%，雖較前季放緩，仍是經
濟成長的重要支柱；營建業受公共及民間工程推進成長加速 8.8%；服務業成長
5.2%，以批發貿易受電子出口帶動表現尤佳。IMF 預期新加坡將持續受惠於 AI、
生技及金融服務發展，預估 2026 年經濟成長達 5.0%、2027 年放緩至 3.0%。 
新加坡金融管理局 10 月政策評估維持新元名目有效匯率政策區間不變，反映 4
月和 7 月提前收緊貨幣政策後，核心通膨符合預期，並預計 2027 年年中隨著供
應驅動型進口通膨放緩和國內生產成本維持低位，核心通膨將明顯回落，預計
2026 年核心通膨率平均為 1.5%至 2.0%，2027 年則為 1.5%至 2.5%。MAS 表示目
前的升值政策將使新元名目有效匯率持續走強，並抑制近期輸入型通膨的上升，

(4)馬來西亞：2026 年第三季經濟成長年增率達 5.3%，其中，民間消費受就業市
場穩定、服務業持續擴張及家庭支出成長帶動而維持韌性；投資則受企業擴產及
資料中心等大型專案推進支撐；此外，全球 AI 及科技產業需求強勁，帶動電子
電機產品出口表現，成為外需的重要動能。從產業面分析，製造業成長加速至
7.5%，為主要成長引擎，係受惠電子、電機及石化相關產業出口增加；服務業、
營建業成長略放慢至 5.4%、6.0%；農業因棕櫚油和畜牧業產量增加從萎縮 3.7%
反彈回升 1.3%，礦業及採石業因原油與凝析油產量下降而衰退 4.9%。整體而言，
馬來西亞經濟呈現「內需穩健、科技出口強勁、投資活絡」的成長格局，IMF 最
新預估 2026 年經濟成長達 5.2%、2027 年放緩至 4.6%。 
六、台灣—AI 帶動出口、投資與消費成長，中經院上修 2026 年經濟預測 
中經院 10 月 16 日公布最新預測，上修 2026 年台灣經濟成長率至 12.16%，
創 1988 年以來新高，主要受惠於 AI 技術快速發展帶動全球科技投資，半導體與
資通訊產品需求強勁，出口維持高速成長，同時民間投資與消費亦同步擴張，經
濟成長動能由內、外需共同支撐。另一方面，由於中東地緣政治風險升溫帶動油
價維持高檔，能源價格外溢效果推升整體物價，中經院將 2026 年 CPI 年增率預
估上修至 2.06%，惟整體通膨仍屬相對溫和。

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國聯準會如市場預期，維持政策利率不變。決策官員仍對通膨保持擔憂，也承
認美國經濟正以「強勁速度」成長。聯準會主席 Kevin Warsh 在會後談話中，並
未明確排除進一步緊縮政策的可能，激勵市場對 12 月升息的預期升溫。數據方
面，美國第三季 GDP 年化成長率達 3.4%，高於前值的 2.2%，亦優於市場預期
的 3.2%，印證美國經濟動能強勁。此外，9 月 PCE 與核心 PCE 符合預期，但凸
顯通膨壓力持續偏高。在「通膨高企」與「經濟強韌」雙重因素驅動下，市場預
期聯準會將於 12 月升息，為美元指數提供有力支撐。惟近期受 AI 產業股票波
動加劇影響，部分資金轉入美債避險，導致美元指數在高檔面臨調節性賣壓。
⚫ 儘管受能源價格走揚影響，9 月歐元區通膨率升至 3.8%，但在薪資壓力維持溫
和，且歐洲央行尚未觀察到第二輪通膨效應全面擴散的情況下，歐洲央行上週決
議維持政策利率不變。通膨回升令市場預期歐洲央行將在下次會議升息，為歐元

提供部分支撐，但考量歐元區經濟成長表現仍落後美國，在美元基本面強勁的抑
制下，歐元反彈力道相對有限。目前歐元最大的潛在風險在於部分國家的債券市
場壓力尚未消散，市場對歐洲財政風險仍保持高度警戒。
⚫ 日本央行維持政策利率於 1.25%不變，並且首次明確表示已達成其 2%的通膨目
標；過去日本央行雖認為通膨改善，但始終強調尚未穩定達標，如今首次正式承
認達成 2%目標，代表未來升息門檻進一步下降。此外，日本央行將經濟成長預
測維持在 0.7%至 0.8%，並小幅上修 CPI 預測，令市場預期日央 12 月有望再次
升息，日本貨幣政策正常化將有助日圓結束疲軟走勢。

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Nov/2/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.390，低點在 32.676，以 32.478 作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 3 分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，市場等待聯準會利率決議，交投偏謹慎觀望，而外
資在前周大幅匯出之後，轉為雙向進出，台幣止跌回穩，最高來到 32.390 的周內高
點；然而周間，美國科技股財報表現失色，台股賣壓沉重，外資賣超台股並再度轉
為大幅匯出，台幣走貶，最低來到 32.676，不過匯價逢高吸引出口商賣壓出籠，加
上央行提供流動性，台幣貶幅收斂，終場在央行調節之下收在 32.478。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣偏弱震盪。就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持利率不
變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，且聯準會表示通膨仍處於高檔，顯示聯準會對通
膨仍保持警戒，市場預期美國利率將維持高檔，台美利差仍維持在相對高位，不利
台幣表現；另一方面，美國科技股財報表現不如預期，引發市場對 AI 投資回報的疑
慮，對台股也帶來壓力。就台灣市場來看，外資持續調節台股並大幅匯出，10 月累
計賣超將近兆元，資金匯出壓力大，不過一方面央行持續進場調節，加上匯價逢高
預計吸引出口商進場拋匯，料將限制台幣貶值速度。綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢
偏弱。

美元兌人民幣匯率走勢

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣持續走升。周初市場等待美國聯準會利率決議，交投偏向觀望，境
外人民幣主要於 6.6600 上方震盪；周間聯準會如預期維持利率不變，美元指數自 102
上方回落，加上中國出口結匯需求持續提供支撐，境外人民幣突破 6.6600 整數關口；
週五人行中間價續創近三年九個月新高，最高來到 6.7138，顯示中國人行允許人民
幣溫和升值的走勢，境外人民幣一度升破 6.6500 整數關口，最高來到 6.6487，終場
收在 6.6528，週線升值 162 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6558，週線升值 146 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣偏升。就中國國內市場來看，10 月官方製造業 PMI 降至 49.3，
重新落入景氣收縮區間，反映中國內需及製造業動能仍有待改善，不過 9 月出口年
增 36.3%，出口商結匯需求持續提供人民幣支撐，加上人行中間價續創近三年九個
月新高，預計將持續帶動人民幣走升；就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持
利率不變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，顯示聯準會對通膨仍保持警戒，市場預期
美國利率將維持高檔，美中利差維持在相對高位，將限制人民幣升值的速度與幅度；
最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，各天期均線向下發散，匯價維持在五日均
線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，顯示空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣
偏升。

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Nov/02/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 上漲。週初，美伊衝突仍陷僵局支撐美元避險買盤，USD/JPY 最
高來到 158.34；週間，聯準會公布利率維持利率不變，USD/JPY 持續在 158 附近盤
整；臨近週末，日本央行公布利率維持不變並釋放偏鷹訊號，USD/JPY 最低來到
156.94，終場收在 157.53，週線上漲 72 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本央行於上周五宣布利
率維持在 1.25%不變，並表示隨著基礎通膨率大致達到 2%，央行必須持續控制通
膨穩定於此目標，且再次強調貨幣政策已進入更為緊縮階段，未來每隔數月即可能
升息。此外，日本央行在季度報告中，亦將截至 2028 年 3 月財年的核心消費者通
膨預估自三個月前預測的 2.4%上調至 2.6%。日本央行偏鷹立場令市場預期日本最
快將於 12 月再次升息，預計將有利日圓表現。二、聯準會宣布維持利率於 3.75%-
4%區間不變，惟因通膨仍處高位，聯準會重申將致力實現 2%的長期通膨區間， 
FedWatch 顯示 12 月升息機率約七成，美元持穩，預計將限縮 USD/JPY 下行空
間。三、就技術面而言，在日線圖中，USD/JPY 各天期均線趨平，近期匯價大致
在 156 至 158 間震盪，匯價暫無明確方向性。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現
區間震盪的可能性較高。

美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Nov/02/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，美伊衝突持續以及法國政經情勢動盪等情況下，
歐元走跌，最低來到 1.1111；週間，聯準會和歐洲央行相繼公布利率決議均維持不
變，歐洲央行發表言論偏鷹派，歐元止跌回升至 1.1214；臨近週末，歐元區公布經
濟數據好壞參半，歐元自高點回落，終場收在 1.1158，週線下跌 41 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行如市場預期宣布
存款利率維持在 2.5%不變，並警告能源成本持續上漲將對通膨造成上行風險，暗示
可能在 12 月再次升息，升息預期升溫預計將支撐歐元走勢。二、歐元區第三季 GDP
年增 1.3%，高於市場預期的 1.1%，顯示在能源價格持續上漲的不利因素下，經濟仍
展現韌性，預計有利歐元表現。三、聯準會宣布維持利率在 3.75%-4%不變，並重申
致力控制通膨的立場。此外，美國公布第三季 GDP 成長 3.4%，優於市場預期的 3.2%，
9 月核心 PCE 年增率自前月的 3%上升至 3.1%，在美國經濟強勁及通膨仍高的情況
下，市場預期聯準會將於 12 月升息一碼，美元持穩，預計將限縮歐元走升空間。四、
技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 各天期均線向下發散，不過近期匯價在多次向
下測試 1.1120 附近均未有效跌破，顯示下方仍有買盤支撐。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD
本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.10.27-115.10.30 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.66% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109R 2.03% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.845%~1.846%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，時序邁入月底，銀行間已大致調整好積數，且央行未明顯
增發定存單，令市場資金充裕。近兩日股市回落，令週五資金需求上揚，惟供
需尚屬平衡，因此短率持穩於近期區間。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.825% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.66%~1.68%，利率大致持平於上週。 
二、 本週台債市場交投依舊清淡，10 年期指標券 A15109R 等殖系統成交在 2.03%，
5 年期指標券 A15108 議價成交在 1.845%~1.846%。 
美債利率自波段高點回落，且央行總裁楊金龍本週立法院發言維持近來對通
膨容忍度變寬之態度，惟市場對央行升息預期並未完全消除，買盤追價動力有
限，預期台債殖利率維持區間整理，關注下週 30 年期公債標售果，預估 5 年
券區間為 1.80%~1.85%，10 年券區間為 1.99%~2.04%。

eMemory Technology Inc  | Asia Pacific
Security demand resilient amid
cloud slowdown
Record earnings: 3Q revenue was NT$1.21bn (+10.4% Q/Q, +27.1% Y/Y) and
attributable net income was NT$639mn (+10.2% Q/Q, +31.2% Y/Y), 2.7% and 1.6%
above MSe, respectively. Operating income beat MSe by 7.0%, while margin reached
63.2%, 2.6ppt above MSe. Royalties rose 15.4% Q/Q, with 12-inch royalties up 20.2%,
versus 3.5% opex growth. PUF security revenue grew 213% Y/Y to 14.5% of sales.

EPS was NT$8.56.
Royalty outlook: Management expects growth to accelerate on higher advanced-
node rates, product ramps and PUF adoption. Customers favor fewer, higher-value
flagship designs, with advanced-node licensing and royalty values several times
those of mature nodes. Management provided no quantified 4Q guidance.
Security expansion: PUFrt adoption in Arm Neoverse CSS V3, BMC, SSD and
networking broadens data-center exposure. Penetration, content per chip and node

migration support growth, while PQC adds license value. Some 3nm designs are in
production, with 2nm qualification and initial adoption targeted for 2027. PUF-
integrated 1T Flash remains in foundry development, with no launch date. PUF
accounts for only 1.8% of royalties, leaving upside dependent on conversion to
production.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
eMemory Technology Inc (3529.TWO)
We derive our price target from a residual income model. We assume an 8.6% cost of equity
(2.0% risk-free rate, 6.0% equity risk premium, and 1.1 beta), a payout ratio of 90%, a medium-
term earnings growth rate of 23.0%, and a terminal growth rate of 4.4%. Based on these
assumptions, our RI model implies an equity value of NT$249bn, or NT$3,330 per share.
Risks to Upside

n Faster-than-expected PUF adoption
n Stronger-than-expected advanced-node OTP migration
n Higher-than-expected royalty expansion from wafer value growth
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected adoption by key customers
n Slower-than-expected PQC adoption
n Increasing geopolitical and trade policy risks

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Document 117: 261117_daiwa_andes

Source PDF: all report pdf/261117_daiwa_andes.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following on our results Memo for Andes, we widen our 
2026E net loss and maintain our Hold (3) rating. Management aims for a 
profitable 4Q26 to cover YTD losses, yet we are unsure how sustainable its 
bottom-line turnaround will be in the near term, given still uncertain timing 
of royalty conversion despite enriched IP and customer portfolios. That 
said, we remain structurally positive on Andes’s outlook, thanks to its 
leading position in the RISC-V architecture well prepared to capitalise on 
the AI cycle from cloud to edge to physical.

What’s the impact: Forecast changes in a mixed bag. We widen our 
2026E net loss for Andes to factor in its higher-than-expected opex YTD, 
but we keep our 2027E EPS largely unchanged to factor in its frugal opex 
control starting from 3Q26. Though bottom-line turnaround looks still 
bumpy in the near term on its suboptimal operating leverage due to still 
suboptimal scale, as a result of still uncertain timing of royalty revenue 
conversion, we merit management’s opex control to speed up bottom-line 
turnaround. This, together with its enriched RISC-V IP portfolio and 
broadened customer base for market-share gain, should help sustain its 
turnaround from 2H27 when topline expands to improve earnings quality 
and investor confidence.

Patience still for a sustainable turnaround. Despite this year’s business 
hiccups on delayed royalty ramp and opex volatility, we remain positive on 
Andes’s structural outlook, given its leading position in the RISC-V 
architecture offering enriched high-performance/low-power compute IP as 
substitutes to Arm and x86 CPU counterparts to capitalise on the secular AI 
cycle, with a broad coverage of applications from accelerated compute and 
embedded control to automotive. Management’s comments support our 
view, as it expects the RISC-V’s open-source ecosystem to drive innovation 
allowing designs of instruction sets tailored to customers’ specific needs, 
which should help enhance customer stickiness while attracting new 
customers for market-share gains. That said, patience may be still needed 
for Andes’s monetisation to bear fruit for a sustainable turnaround.

What we recommend: Despite our forecast revisions, we raise our 12M 
TP to TWD240 (from TWD180), still based on DCF methodology (WACC: 
18%, terminal growth: 3%) which we believe is appropriate for a startup IP 
company such as Andes. Given the still bumpy road to any sustainable 
turnaround, we maintain our Hold (3) call with key upside/downside risks of 
any faster RISC-V ecosystem build/further delays of royalty ramp.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E EPS are 29-48% above the consensus 
forecast likely due to our lower opex assumptions for Andes.

17 November 2026

Near-term turnaround still looks bumpy

➢ 2026E net loss likely to widen on opex volatility but … 
➢ … no harm to structural turnaround from 2H27 
➢ Maintaining our Hold (3) rating with a higher 12M TP of TWD240

Company profile 
Founded in 2005, Andes is an embedded microprocessor intellectual property (IP) provider with 
solutions in high-performance and low-power 32/64-bit processors and associated SoC platforms. Its 
solutions can be adopted for diverse applications such as IoT, 5G, AI, Bluetooth/WiFi devices, gaming 
and automotive. As a founding member of RISC-V International, Andes focuses on developing 
processor IPs based on the RISC-V instruction-set architecture while its mature V3 architecture is the 
main contributor of its royalty revenue.

Andes Technology (6533 TT): 17 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 17 Nov 2026

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
Andes’ Board of Directors consists of seven members, including three independent directors,
representing approximately 43% of the total board composition, exceeding the regulatory
minimum requirement of 20%. The roles of Chairman and President are held by separate
individuals, reflecting a clear division of leadership responsibilities.
Currently, the Board includes one female director among the seven seats. Andes acknowledges
that gender diversity at the board level remains an area for improvement and is actively
committed to strengthening diversity and inclusion as part of its ongoing governance
enhancement efforts.
Capital management 1
As a semiconductor IP provider without physical manufacturing operations, Andes does not
require significant capital expenditure for factories. Instead, its primary capital needs are related
to ongoing investment in research and development to support new IP development and capture
emerging market opportunities. From this perspective, we believe Andes is inclined to retain a
portion of its earnings to fund future IP innovation rather than fully distributing profits to
shareholders.
Andes did not distribute dividends prior to 2019. In 2019, it paid out 72% of its 2018 net profit as
dividends. Subsequently, the dividend payout ratio remained high at approximately 90% during
the period from 2020 to 2022. However, the payout ratio declined sharply to 21% in 2023 based
on 2022 net profit, reflecting a more conservative capital allocation approach.
Due to weakened profitability, Andes did not distribute dividends in 2024 or 2025, as earnings
were insufficient to support shareholder payouts. Management has prioritized financial flexibility
and the preservation of resources to navigate challenging market conditions and sustain long-
term R&D investment.
Related party &
transaction 1
Before July 2021, Andes conducted related party transactions with MediaTek (2454 TT) given
each company's chairman was the same person. MediaTek was Andes' largest customer with a
31% revenue contribution in 2013. Its revenue contribution has gradually declined in past years
to a single digit percentage in 2021. Since Andes' board of directors' election in July 2021, its
chairman and MediaTek's chairman are no longer the same person. Andes currently does not
have any meaningful related party transactions.
S Supply chain management 1
As a semiconductor IP provider without physical products, Andes mainly procures softwares &
services from its suppliers. Andes implements supplier management policies to ensure that there
is no violation of corporate social responsibility policy in the procurement contract with its
suppliers.
E Water & wastewater management 1
Andes is a semiconductor IP provider without any physical products. Thus, it does not have on-
site manufacturing activities to weigh on the environment. It has set up a goal to reduce water
consumption by 2% every year through measures such as installing faucet water savers and
promoting the practice of saving water.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1 Andes is a semiconductor IP provider without any physical products. Thus, it does not have on- site manufacturing activities to weigh on the environment.
E GHG emissions 1
Andes is a semiconductor IP provider without any physical products. Thus, it does not have on-
site manufacturing activities to weigh on the environment. However, the company still applies
measures to reduce power consumption for its operations, such as turning off lights one hour per
day during the lunch break. It has set up a goal to reduce its total electricity consumption by 1-
2% every year.

Global Research
Initiation of Coverage
Elite Material
Manifesting AI-driven upcycle; initiate at Buy
Initiate on TW CCL - EMC: major beneficiary of  structural CCL material upgrade
We initiate EMC with a Buy rating and PT NT$5,000. The company is a global CCL leader
that enables AI systems with high-speed and low-loss materials. We see booming GPU/
ASIC demand driving not just CCL consumption but also a shift toward higher-grade

CCL products to meet high-speed and signal integrity requirements. M8 CCL has
become standard for Blackwell and will migrate to M9/M10 for Feynman.  We anticipate
these structural upgrades and capacity expansion to support  62% sales CAGR   through
'26-'28E; and anticipate product mix shift toward  M7+ to drive margin expansion.
Resilient fundamentals underpinned by strong positioning
Our industry studies suggest EMC is well positioned across AI GPU/ASIC projects. It was

the dominant supplier for Nvidia Hopper OAM/UBB boards. Doosan has begun to supply
compute tray from Blackwell, while EMC and SYTECH supplies the switch tray. We
believe EMC is also one of the major suppliers for Google TPU (splitting with Panasonic)
and AWS Trainium (other suppliers including a TW CCL supplier with potential new
entrants such as SYTECH and Nan Ya Plastics). We see AI platforms ramping up
production in H226 that are enlarging the supply/demand imbalance, driving further

CCL pricing uplifts. We believe EMC's capacity scale and major projects' share allocation
backed by its upstream sourcing will support its growth realization of strong AI demand.
Investing in M8+ for future growth with margin expansion
As a major supplier for  GPU and ASIC platforms, EMC is expanding capacity to capture
future growth. The company announced an investment total of NT$98.6bn through
2027-2030F to add capacity across Taiwan, Mainland China, and Malaysia, where its

total capacity will increase from 5,850k sheets/month in 2026F to 15,250k sheets/
month by 2030F, and these additions would be supporting M8+ CCL demand. M7+
increased from 55–60% of product mix in Q126 to 65–70% in Q326. Looking ahead,
management guides for M7+ to reach 70–80%  in 2027F as AI server specs continue to
move up the material ladder. We expect earnings to grow at 82% CAGR through 2026-
2028E, outpacing topline growth of 62% for the same periods, supported by favourable

product mix shift. Meanwhile, EMC is attempting to build ABF substrate CCL capacity
and going through qualification in H226. We believe high-end CCL remains its key focus
and note that these capacity additions are fungible for M8/M9 high-speed CCL
production, and we have not yet factored in ABF substrate CCL business potential.
Valuation: Initiate at Buy with PT$5,000
In light of market concerns on AI slowdown, we note that our estimates are below

consensus factoring in slower capacity ramp (10-30% lower than announced plans).
Our NT$5,000 PT is based on 12x 2028E PE, which we think is conservative at the low-
end of its  historical trading range of 7-20x. Despite more conservative estimates, still see
attractive risk-reward back by  37%'26-'30E earnings CAGR, implying only 0.3x PEG.

Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Is favourable pricing environment and current CCL industry upcycle sustainable?
We continue to see AI CCL content value increases as computing and networking/interconnect
demands surges. Extended equipment lead time and qualification process along with increasing
manufacturing challenges are also suggesting more constrained upstream inputs. We expect

persisting CCL industry shortage to drive better pricing power and believe high-end CCL have
entered a spec-driven structural upgrade cycle, not a temporary commodity uptick.
Q: Could high-end CCL drive the next phase of growth for Elite Material?
Yes. Among Taiwanese suppliers, we believe EMC is best positioned to capitalise on rapid AI
deployment based on its competitive high-end material offerings across GPU/ASIC platforms. We
expect EMC will continue to benefit from growing AI-driven computing/networking demand and

high-end CCL spec upgrades as a key supplier for major GPU and several US cloud service providers'
ASIC CCL materials. We are positive on its solid business fundamentals and see its business scale,
customer exposure, further pricing uplifts, favourable product mix shift could drive strong earnings
expansion in the next few years.
Among CCL supply chain, we believe EMC is best positioned for further re-rating given its leading
position in high-end CCL materials with capacity scale, high-end product offerings, and sourcing

capability. The company has board customer exposure among major GPU and ASIC platforms, best
positioned to capitalize AI-driven spec upgrades.
Top 6 CSP capex spending has revised up another leg, strengthening visibility into AI-related CCL
demand. Hyperscaler capex grew 25% QoQ/ 101% YoY in Q326 to US$252.7bn, with UBS 2026/27E
forecasts revised up 2%/18% to imply +96%/+74% YoY growth to US$897bn/US$1.6tn.
Meanwhile, the pricing backdrop remains supportive,   with Taiwanese CCL suppliers raising prices by

10-30% QoQ in Q126 and Q226, followed by further double-digit increases in August and
September. EMC’s latest commentary reinforces this trend,  noting 5-20% price increases across its
portfolio in Q426F and further upside possible should the supply-demand mismatch persist.
EMC’s share price has fallen 56% from its Aug peak (vs. TAIEX -21%), amid the broader tech sell-off
and rising concerns over slower AI spending. The stock now trades at 10x/7x our 2027/2028E P/E,

below its long-term historical avg. of 13.5x and the lower-end of the  AI cycle 10-48x, despite our
'27/'28 EPS estimates already  c.4%/4% below consensus. We believe current valuation increasingly
prices in slower AI demand, while giving insufficient credit to EMC’s technology leadership and
continued M8/M9/M10 upgrades, which we believe warrant a premium to CCL peers. Reflecting
recent market sentiment, we conservatively value EMC at  12x 2028E P/E, around its historical

median. This remains attractive given 37% 2026-30E earnings CAGR and implies  0.3x PEG.

Elite Material  was founded in Taoyuan in 1992 and started as a conventional FR-4 copper clad thin
core laminate and prepreg manufacturer. Over time, EMC has established its global presence as the

world's largest supplier of halogen-free high-speed CCLs with its leading technology expertise. In
2025, infrastructure applications, including servers, switches, and LEO satellite accounted for 65-
70% of revenue, followed by mobile devices (20-25%), and automation/industrial/others (5-10%).

Company Description

### Page 3

Elite Material UBS Research
business overview
Founded in 1992, EMC  started as a manufacturer of conventional FR-4 CCL and
prepregs used in printed circuit boards. The company began moving up the value chain
with its entry into HDI in 1997, followed by  the introduction of halogen-free materials
licensed from Hitachi Chemical in 2002, allowing EMC to establish a leading position in
handset HDI applications. Smartphones subsequently became EMC's core franchise with

the company sharing c70% market share and becoming a key supplier to Apple as well
as leading Android brands.
EMC began its next major product transition in 2017, when it entered the high-speed
digital materials market with ~0% market share. Over the following seven years, the
company expanded its low-loss material portfolio and customer qualifications across
high-speed switches, general servers and cloud infrastructure, before benefiting from
the rapid growth of AI servers and CSP ASIC platforms. EMC’s high-speed digital market

share reached 28% in 2023, when it became the global No.1 supplier, and increased
further to 38.6% in 2025. This transition has materially reshaped its revenue mix:
infrastructure increased from around 30% of revenue in 2019-21 to 75-80% currently,
while handset exposure declined from 50% to 10-15%. EMC’s customer base has
consequently broadened from handset-centric exposure toward leading networking
and cloud customers, including NVIDIA-related AI platforms and major CSP ASIC

programs. Building on its HDI expertise, EMC further expanded into IC substrate
materials in 2020, and has grown its market share to 1.6% by 2025 where it is now the
only non-Japanese/Korean player with substrate material technology expertise.

HDI leadership underpins EMC’s longstanding consumer-tech franchise
EMC has historically been the global leader in mobile-device applications, with 70%

market share in mobile-phone CCL and over 80% in 5G smartphone CCL. Mobile
devices remain a meaningful 10-15% of EMC's Q326 sales, supported by  its
longstanding leadership in HDI materials, where it has ranked No.1 globally for more
than 20 years with over 70% share. EMC began developing conventional HDI in 1997,
followed by Anylayer HDI in 2010 and mSAP in 2017, progressively enabling finer lines
and higher circuit density. Unlike high-layer-count (HLC) boards, which primarily add

layers to accommodate more circuitry, HDI uses microvias, finer routing and sequential
lamination to pack more interconnects into a smaller board area, making it particularly
well suited to smartphones, tablets and notebooks where miniaturization is critical. The
repeated lamination and finer structures also place greater demands on CCL, including
dimensional stability, resin-flow control, low CTE, CAF resistance and adhesion, creating
meaningful materials know-how rather than simply a different PCB process. This

expertise helped EMC establish an early position in the Apple supply chain, when
handheld devices rose to  50% of sales in 2016.  EMC’s  historical smartphone customer
base has included Apple, Samsung, Huawei, HTC and Nokia. While consumer tech is no
longer EMC's growth focus, we view it as a stable and strategically important revenue
base, complementing the companies rapidly expanding infrastructure focus.

### Page 4

Figure 3: EMC's technology advancements
Source: Company data, courtesy of EMC
Riding the AI-driven CCL upgrade cycle, with M9 extending high-end leadership
AI infrastructure has become EMC’s core growth engine, with infrastructure reaching
roughly 75-80% of revenue in Q326 and growing 183% YoY, driven primarily by
accelerating AI server and high-speed networking demand. EMC has translated this
demand into a leading position in high-end materials, capturing 38.6% of the global

high-speed halogen-free CCL market in 2025, which should enable the company to
continue outgrowing the broader market through both share gains and content
expansion. Industry forecasts cited by the company call for high-end CCL market to
grow at a 40% CAGR in 2024-2027F and AI CCL at a 178% CAGR in 2025-2027F, while
UBS expects hyperscaler capex to grow +96%/+74% YoY in 2026/27E. As AI spending
broadens across both  GPUs and ASICs, we expect EMC to benefit from rising system

volumes as well as increasing CCL content and value per system.
The latter is driven by a structural migration toward higher-grade, lower-loss materials as
data rates and PCB complexity increase. M7-and-above extreme/super low-loss products
already account for 65-70% of EMC's revenue in Q326, with management expecting
the mix to reach over 70% in 2027. Based on our industry checks, the economic value
creation is non-linear across the material stack. While M7 typically carries a 15-50% ASP

premium over M6, M8 pricing is roughly twice that of M7. The largest step-up occurs at
M9, where ASPs are approximately 5-6x those of M6 and GMs exceed 50% versus
roughly 25-30% for M6 and over 30% for M7. Consequently, EMC's earnings sensitivity
to high-end mix expansion is substantially greater than revenue growth alone would
imply.  M9 represents the next leg of this upgrade cycle. EMC was the first supplier to
complete customer qualification from NVIDIA and expects volume shipments to begin in

H226. Management expects M9 to contribute a mid- to high-single-digit percentage of
revenue in H226 before reaching double digits in 2027. EMC is still the first source for
M9 grade applications  including selected NVIDIA Rubin  boards and next-generation
hyperscaler ASIC platforms, the competitors include potential second source Shengyi
and ITEQ who have also passed qualifications.
Nvidia: sustaining a strong position across successive platform upgrades. EMC’s

position in AI CCL was established in 2023, when it became the primary supplier of high-
speed CCL for Nvidia’s H100 GPU server boards. We believe EMC’s prior track record in
high-end HDI helped position it well as GPU boards moved toward greater layer counts,
multiple lamination and more demanding signal-integrity requirements. Nvidia's
architecture has evolved since then and the supplier landscape has become more
differentiated by board. For GB200/GB300, Doosan is the primary compute-tray CCL

supplier, while EMC is the main supplier for the switch tray, alongside Shengyi. Moving
into Vera Rubin, EMC is positioned for CPX/midplane and switch-tray applications, while
Doosan remains dominant in the compute tray. We believe its over 50% share in
NVIDIA's server CCL should remain stable as it benefits from the annual cadence
upgrades.

### Page 5

ASIC: broad hyperscaler exposure reduces reliance on Nvidia. EMC holds roughly
70% share of ASIC-server CCL, supported by exposure across leading hyperscaler
programs. Beyond Nvidia, EMC is benefiting from the continued ramp of custom silicon
at AWS, Google and Meta, alongside a migration to higher-specification materials from
M7 to M8/M8+. Key growth drivers include AWS Trainium 3, Google TPU v8 shipments
from H226, and expanding deployments of Meta's in-house accelerators. With multiple

custom-silicon platforms scaling concurrently, we expect ASIC demand to remain a
meaningful contributor through 2027, further broadening EMC's participation in
hyperscaler AI spending.
Switch: 800G/1.6T migration drives multi-year upgrade cycle
Switch-related materials account for roughly 15-20% of EMC’s infrastructure revenue.
As the connectivity backbone of AI clusters and cloud data centers, switches are seeing
rapidly rising bandwidth requirements, driving the transition from 400G toward 800G

and ultimately 1.6T. 400G remains the largest contributor today, while 800G began
ramping in 2025 and is expected to take around three years to become mainstream, and
1.6T adoption should begin in 2H26 on a similar trajectory. We expect these overlapping
upgrade cycles to support both volume growth and richer material content over the next
several years.
LEO satellite adds a niche but scalable high-speed CCL opportunity
LEO satellite has developed into a meaningful niche within EMC’s infrastructure

portfolio, accounting for roughly 6% of 2025 revenue, providing  materials across
satellite equipment, ground receiving stations, and user terminal equipment. EMC
began targeting the segment in 2023 and entered mass production for LEO satellite
materials in 2024, alongside completion of next-generation product qualification. The
opportunity fits well with EMC’s existing strengths in high-speed, low-loss laminates
and HDI, as satellite boards require strong signal integrity, thermal reliability and

compact, high-density designs.  Demand is supported by continued constellation
deployment from two major US customers, while material specifications are also
upgrading from M6 to M7 as transmission speeds increase, providing additional CCL
content per satellite. Key competitors include US-based Isola which has an established
presence in aerospace and satellite-grade high-performance laminates. We  see LEO as a
relatively small but differentiated growth driver that broadens EMC’s infrastructure

exposure beyond AI servers and switches.
Substrates represent an emerging growth leg with significant share gain
potential
Substrates remain a relatively small part of Elite Material's business today, but the
company has steadily expanded its market share since entering the market in 2020 to
1.6% in 2025. IC substrate laminates carry significantly higher technical barriers than
conventional PCB laminates, with demanding qualification requirements and a

concentrated supplier base historically dominated by Japanese and Korean vendors
including Resonac, Mitsubishi Gas Chemical (MGC), and Doosan  etc. Ongoing substrate
material shortages have led several customers to qualify alternative suppliers, creating
an opportunity for EMC to penetrate programs supported by leveraging its longstanding
HDI and mSAP expertise as a natural pathway into substrate materials. We believe the
company is currently qualifying materials for AI ASIC-related substrate applications, with

initial shipments potentially commencing following customer approval. Looking
forward, EMC's first milestone is to reach 5% substrate market share, followed by 10%,
with both targets expected to be achieved within the next 12-24 months. We believe
successful execution against these milestones would establish substrates as a
meaningful incremental growth driver and further expand EMC's exposure to AI
infrastructure content growth.

### Page 6

EMC consists of three main business segments: Infrastructure, Mobile Devices, and
Automotive/Industrial.
1. Infrastructure (75-80% of Q326 revenue):
 AI Server (55-60% of Infrastructure)
 General Server (15-20% of Infrastructure)
 Switch (15-20% of Infrastructure)
 LEO satellite (5-10% of Infrastructure)
2. Mobile Devices (10-15% of Q326 revenue). Mobile device applications include
smartphones & tablets, notebooks and memory modules. These products utilize

HDI PCB designs to support device miniaturization, higher circuit density and high-
speed signal transmission.
3. Automotive/Industrial (5-10% of Q326 revenue). Automotive and industrial
applications include autonomous driving (ADAS), electric vehicles (power
conversion and charging), medical devices and industrial electronics, which require
high-reliability PCB materials for demanding operating environments.
Ongoing capacity expansions across Taiwan, Mainland China and Malaysia, and

a unique US production presence
EMC was founded in Taoyuan, Taiwan in 1992, where its headquarters and original CCL/
prepreg manufacturing base remain today. In 2005, EMC added its Hsinchu plant to
provide mass-lamination services for PCB customers requiring outsourced capacity. The
company has since built three major production sites in China, Kunshan in 1999,
Zhongshan in 2005, and Huangshi in 2019, serving Northern/Eastern, Southern and
Central China, respectively. In 2024, EMC established its Penang, Malaysia plant, its first

production base in Southeast Asia. In the US, EMC acquired Arlon EMD in 2020, making
it the only Asian CCL supplier with an active US production presence. In July 2026, EMC
raised planned investment in the site to US$69mn, with the expanded facility targeted
to commence operations from H226.
Management has laid out a substantial multi-year capacity expansion plan to capture
robust AI infrastructure demand and sustained tightness in high-end CCL. In Oct 2026,

the board approved an additional ~NT$98.6bn capex budget for phased factory
expansion across Taiwan, Mainland China and Malaysia, underscoring the scale of
EMC’s capacity build-out. Laminate capacity is expected to increase from 5.85mn
sheets/month in 2026E to 8.70mn in 2027E (+49% YoY), 10.45mn in 2028E (+20%
YoY), 11.95mn in 2029E (+14% YoY) and 15.25mn by 2030E (+28% YoY), implying a
~27% CAGR over 2026-30E.
EMC has nevertheless adjusted the phasing of certain expansion plans as it encountered

longer-than-expected expansion lead times in Taiwan. Most notably, the initial 300k
sheets/month Guanyin addition, previously targeted for Dec 2026, has been pushed
back by ~6 months due to labor/material constraints and longer government safety
inspections, while a further 600k sheets/month addition planned for 2028 has shifted to
2029. By contrast, capacity expansion at Kunshan, China can proceed more quickly.
Demand visibility remains robust, with signed LTAs exceeding 9.4mn sheets of

incremental capacity and supporting full utilization through 2028. Customers are
increasingly entering multi-year LTAs to secure high-end CCL supply, while management
continues to secure LTAs and verbal commitments beyond 2028 and sees customer
demand extending through 2030.

### Page 7

Shareholder structure:
Publicly listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in Nov 1998, EMC is chaired by Ting-Yu
Tung who directly  holds ~1.5% of outstanding shares. Related investment vehicles Yu
Chang Investment and Yu Sheng Investment together hold ~10.7%, bringing the
chairman/family-related holding to 12%. FINI ownership peaked at ~55% in 2017 amid
strong smartphone/HDI demand, before declining on smartphone weakness and Apple
SLP share loss. It bottomed at ~24% in 2024 amid concerns over Blackwell CCL share

loss to Korean competitors, but has since recovered to ~41% (vs Taiex's FINI holding
47%), supported by stronger-than-expected AI server/ASIC and high-end CCL demand.

Financial analysis
Q326 earnings beat on strong GM expansion and high-end mix. EMC delivered
record-high Q326 revenue of NT$60.9bn, up 29% QoQ and 142% YoY, driven by
strong infrastructure demand, price increases and favorable product mix. Gross margin
expanded 3.8ppts QoQ to 37.7%, with pricing and mix each contributing roughly half
of the improvement, while operating margin reached 30.7% and EPS rose to NT$40.47.
Infrastructure remained the core growth engine at 75-80% of revenue, with sales up

32% QoQ/176% YoY, while  full utilization underscored continued supply-demand
tightness amid strong high-end CCL demand.
Q426 to see continued pricing and mix momentum. For Q426E, EMC guided for
15-20% QoQ revenue growth and 38.0-39.5% GM, supported by continued price
increases and favorable product mix. By application, management expects infrastructure
revenue to grow ~20% QoQ, mobile to remain flattish, and automotive/industrial/
others to decline 5-10% QoQ, with the latter reflecting lower volumes as capacity is

reallocated toward high-speed CCL. We forecast Q426 revenue of NT$69.4bn (+14%
QoQ), GM to increase 0.5ppts to 38.2% and EPS of NT$47.03 (+16% QoQ). EMC plans
5-20% price increases across its portfolio in Q4, with pricing reflecting raw-material cost
increases; management also noted further upside to pricing if the supply-demand
imbalance persists.

Revenue and GM and EPS forecasts for 2027/2028E
We forecast EMC’s revenue to grow 80%/45% YoY in 2027/2028E, supported by
substantial capacity expansion, continued pricing and sustained high-end CCL demand.
Capacity is expected to increase ~49%/20% YoY in 2027/2028, with new capacity
coming online progressively and management expecting utilization to remain full

through 2028. EMC is accelerating investment to capture this demand, with capex rising
from NT$28bn in 2026 to NT$53bn in both 2027 and 2028, although FCF is expected to
remain negative for two years before turning positive in 2028-2029. Management
indicated signed LTAs already exceed 9.4mn sheets of incremental capacity, providing
strong visibility into the ramp, while substrate laminate represents additional upside,
with a new ASIC project expected to complete qualification by end-2026 and begin

shipments in 2027.
Pricing and product mix should provide additional growth on top of the capacity ramp.
EMC expects high-end CCL tightness to persist for the next two years, as extended
equipment lead times constrain the pace of new supply, while E-glass tightness could

### Page 9

persist until H227. Management sees further room for price increases if the supply-
demand imbalance persists. Product mix should provide another ASP and margin
tailwind, with M7+ expected to rise from 65-70% of Q326 sales to 70-80% in 2027. M9
began shipments in H226 and is expected to enter MP in 2027, while M10 qualification
is targeted for completion by end-2026, enabling initial shipments in H127 ahead of MP
in H227. With  typical generational upgrades carrying at least a 30% price increase, we

forecast GM to expand from 35.7% in 2026E to 40.5%/42.0% in 2027/2028E and
OPM to rise from 28.6% to 34.2%/36.2%, reflecting continued pricing, richer high-end
mix and operating leverage, driving EPS of NT$280.82/430.74, up 116%/53% YoY,
respectively. Our 2027/2028E EPS estimates are c.4%/4% below consensus, reflecting
more conservative utilization and pricing assumptions to account for potential downside
from slower AI/NVIDIA demand, while we remain positive on EMC’s technology

leadership and high-end product positioning.

Sensitivity analysis
Quantifying earnings downside  risks if AI spending slows down from 2028
The US Department of Commerce measures announced on 16 November seek to
prevent China AI-chip controls from being circumvented through third-country data

centers. The rules extend controls beyond the physical shipment of advanced chips to
who ultimately accesses the compute, while introducing licensing requirements for
advanced-chip exports, re-exports and transfers to Singapore, Malaysia, Indonesia and
Thailand, as well as Brazil, which BIS identifies as presenting higher-than-normal
diversion risk. Chips destined for US-headquartered companies or US-parented
subsidiaries are exempt from this country-level requirement, subject to reporting

conditions. The rules do not altogether prohibit ASEAN AI investment, but raise licensing
and compliance hurdles for parts of the data center ecosystem (LINK).  This raises
concerns on first-order impacts of likely to be delayed GPU deployment and slower data
center build-outs. While we believe AI data center build-out remains supported by cloud
computing power growth, enterprise AI and inference, we note it is constrained by data-
sovereignty and latency requirements, as well as power and land supply limits. We

### Page 10

believe the market continues to expect a strong 2027 but is now more cautious for
2028. We think some market expectations of a down year might be too conservative as
the UBS team has been noting that operating cash flow should support sustainable
capex for 2028. That being said, we still see some sensitivity around potential acts that
might cap hyperscalers from securing permitted sites and/or receiving power/water
permits.
Below we examine the potential downside risks into 2028 earnings if AI spending

materially slows down vs our current base case scenario. Our key assumptions and
findings are as follows:
 If capacity expansion plan falls short of expectations...: The company has
announced plans to expand CCL monthly capacity from 4.95mn sheets in 2025 to
10.45mn/15.25mn sheets by 2028F/2030F. Our current model assumes its CCL
monthly capacity of 10.08mn sheets in 2028E, and note that if capacity additions
were only 50%/30%/20%/10% executed compared to our base case scenario,

this will imply potential 2028E earnings downside of 25%/35%/39%/43% to our
current estimates.
 ...and pricing environment becomes less favourable: We currently expect to
see AI CCL content value increases driven by computing and networking/
interconnect demands; and extended equipment lead time and qualification
process along with increasing manufacturing challenges are also suggesting more
constrained upstream inputs. That being said, if pricing power starts to weaken on

AI spending slowdown, assuming pricing falls short of our current estimates by
5%/10%/15%/20%, on top of lowered capacity, this will imply potential earnings
downside of 32%/45%/53%/60% to our current 2028E estimates.

EMC’s 12-month forward P/E historically traded below 20x prior to 2023, before re-

rating with the onset of the AI cycle. The re-rating accelerated from 2025 through H126,
driven by 1) EMC’s leadership in M8 and expectations that this would extend into M9; 2)
growing exposure to hyperscaler ASIC programmes and expectations of regaining a
leading supplier position in NVIDIA’s Rubin platform; and 3) rising CCL content per
system as AI architectures adopt higher-specification materials and more complex board
designs. Expectations for CCL price increases and EMC’s technology and capacity

advantages provided further support in H126, taking its forward P/E to a historical peak
of 47x in April 2026. Since the AI cycle began, EMC has traded within a roughly 10-48x
forward P/E range, averaging around 20x.
The subsequent de-rating has been equally significant. EMC’s share price has fallen 56%
from its August peak, versus TAIEX down 21%, amid broader tech-sector selling, rising
concerns over slower AI spending, and uncertainty over whether PTFE adoption in

NVIDIA’s next-generation platforms could challenge EMC's high-end CCL positioning.
At NT$2,810 as of 20 November 2026, EMC trades at 10x/7x our 2027E/2028E EPS,
below its long-term historical median of 13.5x and well below its c.20x post-2023
average. Importantly, our 2027/2028E EPS estimates are already c.4%/4% below
consensus, partly reflecting a more conservative view amid rising AI demand concerns.
We believe the current valuation largely reflects these concerns, but gives insufficient

credit to EMC’s sustained technology leadership and earnings growth from continued
high-end material migration. Management expects M7+ to reach 70-80% of sales in
2027, versus 65-70% in Q326, while M9 is expected to enter mass production in 2027
and M10 could begin initial shipments in H127.
Our NT$5,000 price target is based on 12x 2028E P/E, at the mid to low end of EMC’s
7-20x historical valuation range. This compares with 8x for TUC versus its 7-17x

historical range and 10x for ITEQ versus its 9-19x historical range. We believe EMC’s
premium is warranted by its high-end material leadership, broader GPU and hyperscaler
ASIC exposure, and technology and capacity advantages. Our target multiple is also
above the 8x average for other CCL suppliers and 7x for PCB suppliers, while closer to
the 10x average for substrate suppliers as EMC expands its substrate CCL business.
EMC is trading at NT$2,810 (as of 20

Nov 2026)

UPSIDE (NT$9,000): We assume 2028E sales growth of 49.3%, driven by 62% CCL

growth, reflecting faster M9/M10 adoption, stronger AI server/switch demand and
sustained pricing. We assume a gross margin of 42.5% and EPS of NT$450.2. We apply
20x 2028E P/E to derive our NT$9,000 upside valuation.
BASE (NT$5,000): We assume 2028E sales growth of 45.0%, driven by 57% CCL
growth, supported by continued AI demand, high-end material upgrades and capacity
expansion. We assume a gross margin of 42.0% and EPS of NT$430.7. We apply 12x

2028E P/E to derive our NT$5,000 base-case valuation.
DOWNSIDE (NT$1,650): We assume 2028E sales growth of 17.2%, driven by 22% CCL
growth, reflecting slower AI server/switch demand, delayed high-end material adoption
and an earlier-than-expected easing in CCL supply tightness. We assume a gross margin
of 36.8% and EPS of NT$253.8. We apply 7x 2028E P/E to derive our NT$1,650
downside valuation.

### Page 14

Elite Material UBS Research
Company Description
Company Description
Elite Material was founded in Taoyuan in 1992 as a conventional
FR-4 copper-clad thin-core laminate and prepreg manufacturer.
Over time, EMC expanded globally to become the world's largest
supplier of halogen-free high-speed CCLs, underpinned by its
leading technology expertise. In 2025, infrastructure
applications, including servers, switches, and LEO satellites,
accounted for 65-70% of revenue, followed by mobile devices

(20-25%), and automation, industrial and other applications (5-
10%).
Industry Outlook
The CCL industry outlook remains positive, supported by AI
server investment and upgrades to high-speed networking
infrastructure. Larger, higher-layer-count PCBs and tighter signal-
integrity requirements are increasing demand for high-
performance, ultra-low-loss laminates, supporting both material
content growth and a richer product mix. Tight supply of
specialty glass fabric and other inputs is supporting laminate

price increases, although rising material costs and supply
constraints remain risks to margins and shipment growth. These
trends should favour suppliers with advanced material
capabilities and reliable access to critical inputs.

Company Description
Elite Material was founded in Taoyuan in 1992 as a conventional FR-4 copper-clad thin-core
laminate and prepreg manufacturer. Over time, EMC expanded globally to become the
world's largest supplier of halogen-free high-speed CCLs, underpinned by its leading
technology expertise. In 2025, infrastructure applications, including servers, switches, and

LEO satellites, accounted for 65-70% of revenue, followed by mobile devices (20-25%), and
automation, industrial and other applications (5-10%).
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value EMC using a PE-based methodology.
Key risks include: 1) slower-than-expected high-end CCL adoption and AI infrastructure
deployments or delays in server upgrade cycles; 2) delays in capacity expansion or production
ramp-up; 3) stronger-than-expected competition and market-share losses in high-end CCL;

and 4) higher raw-material costs and weaker-than-expected cost pass-through to customers.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

ways. Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that

貿聯-KY(3665) 
買進, TP 2,900 (潛在報酬 90.79%)  
買進 
Price(11/13):1,520 
Target Price：2900

高毛利產品合計來到 59%，此外營運槓桿帶來的效益使本業獲利
率創歷史新高。Interplex 已完整併表，單月貢獻金額來到 6700
萬美金，除了擴大產品線外，合併後同樣有助於加深既有客戶的合
作關係。展望 2027 年，HPC 事業將迎來產品價量齊揚，尤其以高
毛利的 Power 產品最為明顯，根據客戶規劃，1.6T AEC 也將逐步
起量，因此可預期 HPC 占比將會再進一步提升，利於整體營運表
現。綜合以上，研究部預估貿聯 2026 年 EPS 76.23 元，2027 年
EPS 131.41 元，目前 PE 為 11.6x，參考歷史 PE 區間為 10-35x，
考量貿聯獲利將維持高成長期，唯獨近期市場擔憂資料中心建置速
度恐受高利率環境而放緩，存在一定 AI 伺服器延宕部屬之風險，
因此研究部下修 PER 至 22x，目標價 2900 元，維持買進。 
⚫ 公司基本資料 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
貿聯-KY(3665)成立於 1996 年，為全球連接線大廠，總部位於美
國矽谷，生產據點則廣泛分布在全球 37 地。產品應用包含數據通
訊、工廠自動化、半導體設備、醫療、車用等，2025 年工業佔 35%、
通訊 42%、車用 11%、電器 11%、其他 1%。銷售主要為美國 20%、
中國 20%、德國 16%、馬來西亞 7%、台灣 3%、其他 34%。

此外 AEC 也正式恢復量產加上 800G 滲透率的提高，同樣帶動

殖利率(%) 0.87 HPC 部門成長 Q+30%，Y+61%，SPE 也延續上半年以來的穩健
成長幅度，Q+11%，Y+70%，兩高利潤事業合計貢獻營收占比進
一步攀升至 59%，Q+3ppts，Y+8ppts，在營收成長帶來的營運槓
桿效益下也反映在營益率創公司歷史新高。

Interplex 正式併表，產品組合有望再度優化 
貿聯 10 月營收 118.5 億元，M+26.9%，Y+80.0%，自 9 月起完成
對 Interplex 的收購，10 月已完整貢獻營收，單月貢獻金額高達
6700 萬美金，約 20 億台幣，由於 Interplex 既有客戶與貿聯本身
有所重疊，然而產品項目卻有所不同，預計在透過集團整合能力下
將進一步推動公司在客戶群內滲透率的提升。此外貿聯本身新規格
的 Power 產品也持續提高出貨，研究部預期產品組合有望再度優
化，進一步推升獲利率表現。綜合以上，研究部預估貿聯 26Q4 營
收 349.1 億元，Q+25.3%，Y+76.5%，毛利率 33.84%，稅後淨利
56.7 億元，Q+32.0%，Y+106.8%，EPS 28.39 元；2026 年營收
1069.3 億元，Y+50.1%，毛利率 31.83%，稅後淨利 152.1 億元，
Y+69.0%，EPS 76.23 元。

2027 年 HPC 產品維持價量齊揚，光通訊產品 2028 年有望發酵 
隨著 Vera Rubin 進入放量，Rack Busbar 升級至 5000A，Power 
Whip 規格從 60A 升級至 100A 且用量增加，1.6T AEC 也將逐步
開始貢獻營收，另外 Interplex 的加入所帶來的產品優化和客戶關
係的綁定，研究部認為貿聯 HPC 事業仍將迎來顯著的價量齊揚增
長。光傳輸領域上貿聯也持續耕耘，除了新富生 MPO 相關產品，
OCP 上與 ams Osram 的合作也意味著公司已著手布局下一代
Scale up 的通訊方案，研究部看好 wide-and-slow 架構在未來的導
入機會以及市場，預計 2028 年後的發酵有望帶來營運成長動能。
綜合以上，研究部預估貿聯 2027 年營收 1608.0 億元，Y+50.4%，

結論 
高毛利產品合計來到 59%，此外營運槓桿帶來的效益使本業獲利率創歷
史新高。Interplex 已完整併表，單月貢獻金額來到 6700 萬美金，除了
擴大產品線外，合併後同樣有助於加深既有客戶的合作關係。展望 2027
年，HPC 事業將迎來產品價量齊揚，尤其以高毛利的 Power 產品最為明
顯，根據客戶規劃，1.6T AEC 也將逐步起量，因此可預期 HPC 占比將
會再進一步提升，利於整體營運表現。綜合以上，研究部預估貿聯 2026
年 EPS 76.23 元，2027 年 EPS 131.41 元，目前 PE 為 11.6x，參考歷
史 PE 區間為 10-35x，考量貿聯獲利將維持高成長期，唯獨近期市場擔
憂資料中心建置速度恐受高利率環境而放緩，存在一定 AI 伺服器延宕部
屬之風險，因此研究部下修 PER 至 22x，目標價 2900 元，維持買進。

3Q26 Review: “조삼모사” Again  
3Q26P 연결 실적은 매출액 6조 943억원(YoY -6.0%), 영업이익 4,520억원(YoY -46.2%, OPM 
7.4%)을 기록하며 컨센서스 대비 연결 매출액(7.5조원)과 영업이익(1.16조원) 모두 대폭 하회했다. 주
요 자회사가 양호한 실적을 기록했음에도 불구하고, 지상방산 실적이 매우 부진했다. 지상방산은 매출
액 1조 2,455억원(YoY -41.0%), 영업이익 217억원(YoY -96.2%, OPM 1.7%)을 기록했는데, 1)3Q 
생산 물량이 4Q 매출 인식되는 흐름 속, 고정비 부담이 컸었고, 2)낮은 수출 비중(3Q 48%, 2Q 58%)
과 국내외 사업의 부진한 믹스(국내 BEP 수준, 해외 OPM 11% 추정), 3)비우호적인 원/달러 환율, 4)
대전 사업장 사고 복구 및 기타 일회성 비용(300억원+160억원, 총 460억원) 등이 반영되었다.   
대전 사업장 정상화 시점과 사업 차질 영향은? 
대전 사업장은 내년 상반기 중 정상화될 예정이며, 보은과 여수 공장에서 생산 보완을 위한 조치가 진
행 중인 것으로 소통했다. 또한 대전 사업장은 국내 물량 위주이며, 수출 납기(천무, 천궁-II 추진체 
등)에는 지장이 없을 것이라고 언급했지만, 기타 사업장 램프업을 위한 인건비 증가 등, 수익성 악화는 
불가피할 것으로 예상된다.  
15%대의 매출 성장 가이던스는 달성이 가능할까? 
보수적으로 접근하는 것이 합리적이라는 판단이다. 당사는 ‘26E 지상방산 매출액을 일부 ‘27~28E로 
이연하면서 -6.1% 하향 조정했다. 사측은 폴란드 EC1 천무 인도 가이던스를 기존 40문에서 60문으
로 상향 조정하는 등 납품 속도가 예정대비 빨라진 것을 언급했다. 다만, 천무 EC1에서 미사일 물량 
비중이 높은 것으로 파악되기 때문에 생산 지연 가능성과 수출입 데이터 상 12월 출하 물량의 매출 인
식 이월 가능성을 반영했다. ‘27E는 폴란드 후속 사업과 루마니아 K9, 호주 레드백 등이 양산 페이즈
에 진입하며 성장세가 지속될 전망이다.    
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 1,480,000원 유지 
실적 쇼크와는 별개로, 미국 K9MH 시제, 스페인 K9, 크로아티아 천무 등 수주는 서프라이즈라고 판
단된다. 분기말 수주잔고는 35조원(수출 비중 74%, 스페인 미포함)으로 중장기 성장성에 대한 이견은 
없다. 불가피한 분기 실적 변동으로 동사의 미래 가치를 예단하는 것은 시기상조라는 판단이다.

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발적 
2026-10-29

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 640,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 285,500 원 
상승여력: 124.2%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 감소 및 환율 하락으로 시장 예상치 하회 
Key: 4Q26~1Q27 부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 수주 모멘텀 전망 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 엔진&FDC 으로 밸류 매력도 충분. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 시장예상치 하회하는 실적 시현  
3Q26 연결 매출액 5조 7,536억원(+30.2% YoY, -9.1% QoQ), 영업이익 8,992억원
(+61.3% YoY, -13.5% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(9,792억원)를 하회했다. 조업일수 감소
(휴가 10 일+중대재해 4일) 및 환율 하락 영향(450 억원)과 더불어, 임단협 관련 예상 비
용 중 75%를 이번 분기 선반영 했기 때문이다. 상선 수주 빈티지는 중형 선박 기준 ‘23 년 
9.6%, ‘24년 65%, ‘25년 25%, 대형 선박 기준 ‘22년 4%, ‘23년 45%, ‘24년 31%, 
‘25년 20%로, 선가 상승효과 지속되는 중이다. 특히 해양 부문에서 루야 프로젝트의 공정
률 확대 효과 및 C/O로 180억원의 환입 발생으로 전 분기 대비 큰 폭의 이익 성장 시현했
다. 최근 키윗과 미국 내 합작 조선소 설립을 추진하며, 미 군수지원함 초도 2척 수주 및 향
후 물량 건조, 그리고 HII와의 함정 선체블록 하도급 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 64 만원으로 하향 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 82 만원에서 64 만원
으로 하향한다. 최근 상선 실적 피크아웃 우려에 따른 조선주 주가 급락으로 멀티플 하향 조
정했다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 
FDC 에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지속되고 있는 중이다. 올해 공시 기
준으로 육상발전 엔진 약 1.6GW 수주한 것으로 알려졌으나, 미공시된 약 1.4GW 까지 포
함 시 총 3.0GW 이상을 수주한 상황이다. 캐파 증설 기준으로 ‘28 년 1.5GW, ‘29 년 
3GW 캐파에 해당하는 물량을 이미 대부분 채웠으며, 기존 계획 대비 빠른 준공 혹은 공정
률 확대, 더 나아가 추가적인 캐파 증설까지 고려할 수준으로 파악된다. 빠르면 올해 연말 
혹은 내년 초부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 본격 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 
전망이다. 현 주가 레벨에서 상승여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 3Q 실적 및 임단협 불
확성 해소된 현 시점에서 적극적 매수 의견 및 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)로 제시한다.

작성자(한승한)는 본 조사분석자료에 게재된 내용들이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 신의성실하게 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다.

HD현대중공업 수정TP(원)

Document 243: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

HD현대중공업의 3Q26 연결 실적은 매출액 5조 8,602억원(yoy +33%, qoq -7%), 
영업이익 9,161억원(yoy +64%, qoq -12%, OPM 15.6%)으로 시장 컨센서스를 하회할 
전망이다. 동사의 3Q26 조업일수는 여름휴가, 추석연휴 및 중대재해 등 영향으로 
qoq -21% 감소했으며 환율 역시 qoq -84원 감소한 영향을 감안해 전분기 대비 
실적을 보수적으로 추정했다. 그럼에도 해양 부문 Trion FPU발 C/O에 따른 일회성 
환입이 예상됨에 따라 일부 이익 상쇄가 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 한편, 동사의 
임단협 부결로 당분기 추가적인 인건비 부담은 제한적일 것으로 판단되며 잠수함 
화재 사고 관련 비용 역시 4Q 이후 반영될 전망이다.

동사는 급격히 커져가는 AIDC향 전력수요 및 발전용 엔진 수주에 대응하고자 9/10 
총 4GW 규모의 4행정 중속엔진 캐파 증설을 위한 신규 투자를 공시헸다. 동사의 
기존 DC향 4행정 중속엔진 수주 단가를 고려 시 GW당 약 1조원의 매출 창출이 
가능할 전망이며 2행정 및 선박용 4행정 엔진 대비 높은 수익성이 예상되는 바 
생산이 본격화될 28년부터 동사 실적 성장성의 핵심 동인으로 작용할 전망이다.

동사에 대해 투자의견 BUY를 유지하며 업종 밸류에이션 디레이팅 및 시장 프리미엄 
조정에 따른 Target Multipe 변동으로 목표주가를 70만원으로 하향 조정한다. 상선 
시장 수주 경쟁 심화로 업종 밸류 조정이 이루어지고 있으나 동사는 엔진 기반 장기 
성장성으로 Peer 대비 상대적 매력도 확보 및 주가 방어가 가능하다는 판단이다.

jini.lee@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 
목표주가 540,000 
하향  
현재주가 
(26.10.28) 
285,500 
조선업종

◼ 글로벌 피어 평균 멀티플 하락을 반영하여 목표주가 하향 
◼ 3Q에 산재한 일회성 요인. 원화강세, 조업일수 감소, 임단협 비용 반영 
◼ 모두의 우려에도 불구하고 본업은 안정적으로 천천히 잘 진행되는 중

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 540,000원으로 -32.5% 하향 
목표주가는 28F EPS 42,343원에 글로벌 조선사 피어 평균 12.8배를 적용해 
산출함. 목표주가 하향은 글로벌 경기 불확실성과 중국 조선소 생산캐파 확장
으로 인한 공급과잉 리스크로 전반적인 산업 멀티플이 하락한 것을 반영함. 
다만 가스선 수주 모멘텀과 발전용 엔진의 수주 및 생산 확대 등에 따른 중장
기 성장 유효

3분기는 원래 비수기입니다 
동사의 2026년 3분기 매출액은 5조 7,536억원(+30.2% YoY, -9.1% QoQ), 영
업이익은 8,992억원(+61.3% YoY, -13.5% QoQ), OPM 15.6%를 기록. 3분기는 
1)원달러 환율 하락(전분기 대비 평균 -79원) 2)연휴로 인한 조업일수 감소(47
일), 3)임단협 관련 추가 발생 가능 비용 75% 반영)의 영향이 가장 컸음

중장기적인 모멘텀은 여전히 살아있다 
[상선] 중국 공급 확대에 따른 선가 및 수주 경쟁 심화는 부담이나 LNG선 발
주 재개 및 노후선 교체 수요는 긍정적. 동사의 고부가가치선 위주의 수주 전
략 방향은 유지될 예정 [해양] 루야 프로젝트 진행률 인식으로 4분기도 양호
한 이익률 기대, 다만 현재 수주 부진으로 블록, 혹은 LNG선 투입을 통해 매
출 하락을 방어하는 방안도 구상 중 [방산] 키윗과의 미국 합작 조선소를 통해 
콘솔탱커, 로로선 등 지원함 건조를 시작으로 전투함까지 확장 준비 [엔진] 국
가전력망 및 데이터센터향 발전 수요 확대, 하이퍼스케일러 직접협의로 성장
성 강화, 수주 확정 및 추진 물량 기준 28년 1.5GW, 29년 3GW 생산 계획 중

Document 257: AI交易抛压暂歇，法股指涨幅扩大至2%，金油齐跌，美股感恩节休市

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI交易抛压暂歇，法股指涨幅扩大至2%，金油齐跌，美股感恩节休市.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

法国CAC 40指数涨幅扩⼤⾄2%；⽇经225指数收盘上涨0.9%，⾸尔综指收盘上涨0.3%。
现货⻩⾦跌0.5%⾄3569美元/盎司；西德克萨斯中质原油跌0.5%⾄83.3美元/桶。
摘要：
在经历数周剧烈震荡后，投资者正重返⼈⼯智能交易，亚太科技股与欧股指同步⾛⾼，市场⻛
险偏好出现回暖迹象。这⼀反弹能否持续，取决于市场对AI投资成本与回报的疑虑能否真正消
退。
26⽇，美股感恩节休市。法国CAC 40指数涨幅扩⼤⾄2%，⽇经225指数收盘上涨0.9%，⾸尔
综指收盘上涨0.3%。MSCI亚太指数上涨1.5%，有望录得11个交易⽇以来的第⼆个单⽇涨幅，
科技股表现居前。
个股⽅⾯，铠侠在东京市场⼤涨13%，此前该公司宣布与英伟达合作探索GPU内存容量扩展；

20%、触及熊市⻔槛后，收复了⼤部分跌幅。周四美国市场因感恩节休市，全球美债现货交易
暂停。此前⼀⽇纽约时段，美债10年期收益率下降8个基点⾄4.85%。

震荡尚未平息 AI信仰仍在重建

建设成本与潜在回报的疑问依然存在。
“我们确实看到了⼀些反弹，”⾹港HashKey Capital合伙⼈Mia Mai表⽰，“但我们还不会称之为全
⾯复苏，主要因为对AI的重新信⼼尚未到来。⼈们仍在观望。”

Document 269: AI都攻克黎曼猜想了，为什么机器人还拧不好一颗螺丝？

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI都攻克黎曼猜想了，为什么机器人还拧不好一颗螺丝？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-26
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

具⾝智能的瓶颈正从⼤脑理解与动作规划，转向真实物理世界中的精细操作。机器⼈不仅
缺乏对接触⼒、摩擦和滑动的感知能⼒，灵巧⼿与机械臂之间的协同、操作技能的训练与
复⽤也尚未成熟。如何打通感知、执⾏与学习，让机器⼈稳定完成复杂操作，成为⾏业规
模化落地的关键。
摘要：
AI都能破解黎曼猜想这种百年数学难题、证明⼤名⿍⿍的代数版P≠NP了，可真让AI指挥机器⼈
叠件⾐服、收拾⼀桌杂物，或者做顿饭，它的动作还是太费劲了。
把⽬光转向⽣产线，机器⼈也很难⼲好⼤量看似简单的⼯作。
汽⻋⼯⼚⾥的线束插拔、电池包装配，家电产线中的精密零部件组装，看起来⽆⾮是对准、握
住、推进⼏步，却需要机器⼈持续感知接触状态、调整⼒度和姿态。
现在，具⾝智能的⼀⼤瓶颈就在于，⼤脑发展⻜快，机器⼈越来越会理解指令、规划动作，却
很难稳定完成精细操作。它需要更灵敏的感知，也需要⼀整套把感知变成操作的能⼒。
今天，⼀⽬科技宣布战略升级，基于触觉感知构建机器⼈灵巧操作⽣态（YIMU xEco ——
Dexterous Manipulation Ecosystem）。

此次升级围绕四层产品能⼒：
⾼维感知：把触觉传感器的感知能⼒进⼀步开发、释放，这是物理世界的⼊⼝；
灵巧操作：把灵巧⼿、机械臂，做成⼀个完整的、可交付的上肢本体；
技能沉淀：⼩脑模型将原⼦动作组合成有⾃适应能⼒的Skill。原⼦动作解决复⽤，Skill解决
任务封装；
开放部署：把“⼈类操作演⽰—仿真建模训练—真机强化学习—部署应⽤”这条链路，打磨成⼀
套开发者⾃⼰能上⼿的⼯具，开放给所有客⼾。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

早在2023年，⼀⽬科技就押注⼀个当时⼏乎没⼈相信的命题——要让机器⼈拥有像⼈⼀样的触
觉。如今，这家公司为什么要从⼀触觉感知出发，进⼀步构建⼀套完整的机器⼈灵巧操作⽣
态？
答案就在于，触觉只是机器⼈感知物理世界的⼊⼝，真正实现灵巧操作，还需要打通上肢硬件
集成、操作技能沉淀与场景开发部署等多个环节，让感知能⼒转化为可执⾏、可复⽤、可规模
化交付的操作能⼒。
机器⼈精细操作，为什么还⼀直卡在⼿感上？
⼈类的⼀双⼿，到底有多神奇？
拧瓶盖、插充电线、叠⾐服，这些动作我们⼏乎不⽤思考就能完成。可对机器⼈来说，轻轻⼀
握、稍微⼀转，想要把握好度，都太难了！
如今，机器⼈能跑能跳，⼤脑也越来越聪明，但⾯对真实场景中的精细操作，依然显得笨⼿笨
脚。⼈类习以为常的⼿感，恰恰是机器⼈尚未真正掌握的能⼒。
以插⼊⼀个接头为例，眼睛能够帮助我们找到接⼝，但真正插进去，还得靠⼿指调整⼒度。碰
到阻⼒，我们会下意识地换个⻆度、轻轻再推。机器⼈主要依靠视觉判断位置，接触部位却容
易被遮挡，摄像头也难以直接感知接触⼒、摩擦⼒和滑动趋势。⼈与机器⼈之间的操作差距，

就藏在⼿指调整的那⼏毫⽶⾥。
这也是触觉的重要性所在，机器⼈需要通过触觉获得接触反馈，才能建⽴“接触—反馈—调整”的
操作闭环。
但给机器⼈装上⼀双有感觉的⼿，难度远超想象。⽬前⾏业存在压阻式、电容式、光学式等多
种触觉技术路线，视触觉凭借⾼分辨率受到关注。
但指尖空间有限，传感器既要⾜够灵敏，⼜要耐受频繁挤压和摩擦，还得兼顾⼩型化、响应速
度与成本。⼀双好⽤的机器⼿，既要感知细微变化，也得经受真实⼯作的磨损。
硬件之外，⼿感该怎么教给机器⼈，同样是难题。触觉数据需要通过真实接触采集，不同传感
器的数据格式缺乏统⼀标准，仿真⼜难以完整还原复杂摩擦和柔性材料形变。机器⼈想掌握更
多精细操作，仍需要积累⼤量真实物理交互经验。
围绕这些难题，⼀⽬科技已在触觉领域深耕多年。

### Page 3

其核⼼产品视触觉传感器，通过柔性材料接触物体后的形变与光影变化，利⽤内部光学器件采
集信息，再经算法转化为压⼒分布、纹理和滑动趋势等物理信号，让机器⼈能够感知原本难以
直接观察的接触状态。
据⼀⽬科技披露，⾃研的F20、F80等触觉传感器已实现量产出货，产品经过508万次循环压
测，出⼚⼀致性达到0.02N量级。相关解决⽅案已进⼊医疗科研、汽⻋及家电装配等场景，涉及
±0.1g微⼩固体试剂称量、线缆装配及重复性化验等任务。
指尖有感觉后，机器⼈还需要⼀套完整的硬件体系
端起⼀杯⽔，这个再简单不过了。可仔细想想，⼿指要握得稳，⼿腕要控制姿态，胳膊还得平
稳移动，哪个环节配合不好，⽔就可能洒出来，机器⼈也是如此。
给指尖装上触觉传感器，只是让它有了感觉；想真正⼲好活，还得让整条胳膊配合起来。抓
取、插拔、装配等精细任务，都需要灵巧⼿、机械臂、关节和执⾏器共同完成。
可在现实中，这套配合远没有想象中容易。⽬前，触觉传感器、灵巧⼿和机械臂往往由不同⼚
商提供。客⼾好不容易采购⻬了零部件，装到⼀起才发现，还需要做结构、电⽓、算法三层适

配。机器⼈还没开始⼲活，⼯程师可能已经忙得不可开交。
更⿇烦的是，单个零部件表现出⾊，拼起来也未必好⽤。⼀双灵敏的⼿，加上⼀条有⼒的胳
膊，未必就能组成⼀套灵巧的操作系统。不同部件之间的协同表现，直接影响整套设备的操作
精度、稳定性和量产交付能⼒。
所以，⼀⽬科技将产品布局从触觉感知延伸⾄完整的上肢执⾏系统，把机器⼈上肢作为完整产
品定义、标定和交付。
⼀⽬科技将指尖⾼维接触感知、灵巧⼿、机械臂、关节和执⾏器整合起来，统⼀考虑结构设
计、接⼝适配和系统标定，希望交到客⼾⼿上的，是⼀套能够更快进⼊真实⼯位、真正执⾏精
细操作的机器⼈上肢。
对客⼾⽽⾔，少⼀些复杂的硬件集成，才有更多精⼒解决⽣产现场的实际问题。
据了解，⼀⽬科技⾸款全掌触觉灵巧⼿YIMU xHand计划在2027年CES亮相。相⽐仅在指尖布
置触觉感知，全掌触觉有望让机器⼈进⼀步感知⼿掌与物体之间的接触状态，为更复杂的抓握
和⼿内操作提供信息。

### Page 4

像搭积⽊⼀样，把操作技能变成可复⽤的智能资产
触觉让机器⼈有了⼿感，完整的上肢让它能够执⾏动作，但距离真正⼲好活，还差⼀个关键环
节：机器⼈得学会如何根据接触反馈，决定下⼀步该怎么动。
⼈⼲活久了会积累⼿感和经验，碰到问题⾃然知道如何调整，机器⼈同样需要让感知、控制模
型与执⾏系统配合起来。
以机器⼈安装灯泡为例，它需要控制机械臂的位置、灵巧⼿的姿态和指尖接触⼒，先稳稳握住
灯泡，再对准灯座，旋转过程中还要根据接触阻⼒不断调整⼒度和⻆度，避免拧偏、打滑或⽤
⼒过猛。
这些⼈类⼏秒钟就能完成的动作，背后可能需要机器⼈反复训练，才能把每⼀步衔接起来。
更让⼈头疼的是，机器⼈好不容易学会了⼀⻔⼿艺，换个地⽅可能就⼜得从头学起。 换⼀种零
件、调整⼀下⻆度，甚⾄更换机械臂和灵巧⼿，原先学会的技能都可能需要重新采集数据、训
练和调试。
这暴露出具⾝智能规模化落地的⼀道难题：机器⼈需要积累的，是可以反复使⽤的本领，⽽⾮
只能完成⼀次的任务。怎么将操作经验抽象为标准化技能，让机器⼈能够跨任务调⽤和组合，

是⾏业亟需解决的关键问题。
因此，⼀⽬科技选择⽤⼩脑模型将原⼦动作组合成有⾃适应能⼒的Skill，试图解决技能复⽤问
题。
这个思路有点像打格⽃游戏：单独学会出拳、闪避只是基础，真正厉害的是把这些动作组合成
⼀套套连招。
原⼦动作负责沉淀可复⽤的基础能⼒，Skill则将相关动作封装成完整任务，让机器⼈⾯对不同
需求时，有机会直接调⽤已有的底层能⼒。
这样⼀来，机器⼈⾟苦练出的操作经验，就有机会成为可调⽤、可组合的智能资产，在更多地
⽅派上⽤场，减少新任务的重复开发。
让更多⼈来教机器⼈⼲活，激活灵巧操作的产业创造⼒
归根结底，想教机器⼈学会⼀项新技能，依然是件很不容易的事。

### Page 5

开发者需要先采集数据，再搭建仿真环境、训练模型，最后还要在真实机器⼈上反复调试，才
能让它真正⼲活。这⼀过程涉及多套⼯具，操作复杂、耗时费⼒，也让不少开发者望⽽却步。
对于企业来说，每让机器⼈学会⼀项新⼯作，都可能意味着新⼀轮的开发投⼊。
因此，⾏业迫切需要⼀套更简单、易上⼿的开发⼯具，让更多⼈能够参与进来。
为了改变这种局⾯，⼀⽬科技希望通过YIMU xStudio，将“⼈类操作演⽰—仿真建模训练—真
机强化学习—部署应⽤”串成⼀套标准化⼯具链。原本需要跨越多套系统的开发过程，有望变得
更加连贯、易⽤。
这套⼯具链想解决的，还有⼤量重复劳动。
假如⼀名开发者已经教会机器⼈完成某种操作，另⼀名开发者⾯对相近任务，就有机会调⽤已
有Skill，再根据实际需要调整、组合，让开发者少花时间重复造轮⼦，把更多精⼒留给真正需要
解决的现场问题。
据了解，⼀⽬科技还规划了社区与云服务平台YIMU xWorld，⽀持设备连接和远程操作，希望
吸引更多开发者参与。
未来，开发者可以围绕具体需求创造、开放和共享Skill，让不同团队积累的操作经验，有机会被

更多客⼾使⽤，从⽽获得商业价值，逐步形成技能持续丰富、应⽤不断扩展的⻜轮。
结语：操作能⼒，成为具⾝智能竞争的新分⽔岭
具⾝⼤脑与机器⼈本体不断进步之后，能否在复杂真实环境中稳定完成精细操作，将进⼀步决
定技术落地的深度与⼴度。
这也是⼀⽬科技押注灵巧操作⽣态的原因。通过打通触觉感知、上肢执⾏、技能沉淀和开放部
署，⼀⽬希望让机器⼈学会的本领能够留下来，让更多客⼾和开发者参与创造操作技能。正如
其所说：“上肢是我们的，场景是客⼾的，⽣态是所有⼈的。”
再把⽬光放远⼀些。今天，机器⼈可以帮助⼈类完成⼯⼚⾥的精密装配、危险操作；未来，它
们或许还能深⼊⼈类难以抵达的地⽅，替我们完成更多探索。
在⼀⽬科技创始⼈李智强的设想⾥，未来，⼈类⾝处地球，机器⼈却能在三亿公⾥外的⼩⾏星
上独⽴完成采样，接过原本需要⼀个团队协作完成的⼯作。
「让⼈类突破⾏动的边界」。遥远的星空⾥，就这样伸出了⼀只来⾃地球的⼿。

### Page 6

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

Document 364: Kimi K3成为最强实验室AI

Source PDF: all report pdf/Kimi K3成为最强实验室AI.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

⽣命科学研发平台Benchling联合Applied Compute、英伟达，利⽤科学家真实实验数据对
Kimi K3进⾏后训练，使其在湿实验排障基准上取得71.8%的成绩，位列该平台已测试模型
⾸位，较基础版提升24.9个百分点，领先对照前沿模型11.3个百分点，整个训练仅耗时两
周。
摘要：
11⽉23⽇，Benchling公布了⼀项实验室AI训练结果：经过后训练的Kimi K3，在湿实验排障基
准BenchBench-Protocol上拿到71.8%，位列该公司已测试模型⾸位。这⼀成绩对应实验⽅案
排障与优化任务，模型由Benchling、Applied Compute和NVIDIA合作完成。
同⼀基准上，基础版Kimi K3得分46.9%，Opus 5.5得分60.5%。后训练版K3领先此次对照中得
分最⾼的前沿模型11.3个百分点，相⽐⾃⾝基础版提⾼24.9个百分点。从46.9%到71.8%，团队
使⽤的材料，来⾃科学家实际做过的实验。
后训练版Kimi K3与前沿模型、基础版K3的得分对照（图源：Benchling）

把实验台上的判断，变成模型的考题
实验条件改变后，⽅案应该怎样调整？结果出现偏差，接下来该检查什么？BenchBench-
Protocol围绕这类实际决策设计，覆盖湿实验⽣物学的9个领域。据Applied Compute介绍，每
道题给模型⼀份已发表的实验⽅案，以及科学家提出的⽅案调整问题。
模型需要读懂⽅案，再对具体条件作出判断。答案按照经过多位博⼠级科学家审核的评分细则
评定。题⽬涉及识别混杂因素、审查实验计划、处理定量调整等场景；评分关注回答是否抓住
影响实验判断的关键关系。
已发表⽅案与真实实验中的调整记录，被整理为带评分细则的任务，再经过专家质检（图源：
Benchling）
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

这些判断有⼀部分留在实验记录⾥。Benchling在发布稿中解释，已发表的实验⽅案通常呈现最
终有效的做法，实验⼈员还经历过尝试、失败和调整。团队希望模型学到的，正是科学家在这
些过程中积累的判断依据。
为此，Benchling从科学专家贡献的真实实验出发，重建当时⾯对的决策，并与专家⼀起制定评
分细则。最终，真实实验被整理为2,000项带评分细则的任务。细则要区分某位研究者个⼈的操
作习惯与有科学依据的推理，让模型学习可⽤于判断的知识。
Benchling披露2,000项任务的来源，以及与科学专家共同制定评分细则的过程（图源：
Benchling官⽹原⽂截图）
1,850项训练，150项留作评估
Applied Compute进⼀步披露了数据划分：1,850项任务⽤于强化学习，150项单独留作评估。
团队同时跟踪公开的BenchBench-Protocol测试套件，并说明这套公开测试题未⽤于训练。训练
任务、留出任务和公开基准，在项⽬中各有⽤途。
这次后训练在Applied Compute的AC2平台上完成。Benchling提供科学数据与领域知识，

Applied Compute和NVIDIA提供训练基础设施及相关技术⽀持。在Applied Compute的基础评
估中，Kimi K3是表现最好的开放权重模型，因此成为这次后训练的起点。
1,850项任务⽤于强化学习、150项留作评估，公开基准另⾏跟踪（图源：Applied Compute官
⽹原⽂截图）
训练的反馈像⼀张逐项打分的阅卷表。⼀个⼤模型评审器根据专家细则，对每项标准给出零
分、部分分或满分，再把加权总分作为强化学习奖励。模型反复作答，训练系统依据这些奖励
更新模型，推动回答更充分地覆盖科学判断要点。
运⾏环境也经过选择。团队先在代表性任务上⽐较了6种智能体运⾏框架，最终采⽤不带⽹⻚搜
索的简单框架，依靠模型⾃⾝推理完成任务。训练过程中，Applied Compute还与Benchling研
究⼈员检查回答和评审⾏为，核对奖励是否对应更好的答案。

### Page 3

Applied Compute披露的运⾏框架选择、逐项评分与训练监测⽅法（图源：Applied Compute官
⽹原⽂截图）
两周之后，K3拿到71.8%
从接收Benchling的任务数据，到得到最终训练检查点，Applied Compute给出的时间是两
周。团队报告，在100步训练中，训练奖励、留出任务得分和公开基准得分都随训练提升；模型
在作答前，也会对实验⽅案展开更深⼊、更⻓的推理。
最终，Applied Compute提供候选检查点的推理服务，由Benchling使⽤⾃⼰的运⾏框架和评审
器进⾏评估。公开BenchBench-Protocol上的最终得分为71.8%。下⽅训练曲线展⽰的是150
项留出任务的得分及训练奖励，分别反映模型在留出题⽬和训练批次上的表现。
100步训练中的150项留出任务得分与训练奖励；上图末端74%对应留出集，71.8%对应公开
BenchBench-Protocol（图源：Applied Compute官⽹交互图截图）
团队还并⾏训练了⼀个更⼩的模型：NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Super VL。后训练后的得分为

41.4%，基础版为38%，同场对照的GPT-6 Luna为39.9%。Applied Compute介绍，团队与
NVIDIA进⾏了多组消融实验，探索较⼩模型在科学任务上的表现。
⼩模型同时被⽤于考察响应速度与推理成本。Benchling称，在初步测试中，后训练版
Nemotron的回答速度约为K3的4倍，推理成本约为K3的五⼗分之⼀。这些效率数据来⾃团队的
初测；Benchling表⽰，将继续提升该模型的科学任务表现。

Document 506: マーケット見通し『向こう１年間の市場見通し』(2026年12月号)

### Page 1

向こう１年間の市場見通し （11月６日時点）

（注）記載された内容は、第一生命ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限りません。
予 想コメント
コメント
株式
日本株は、６月までＡＩ・半導体関連株主導で上値を追った後、原油価格の高止まりと主要中銀の利上げを受けて
下落した。もっとも、ＡＩ関連株の業績見通しは日米ともに上向いている。米国のＡＩ投資に対する規制強化は懸念
されるが、株価は業績見合いの持ち直しが期待される。
ドル円
ＦＲＢは９月に利上げを再開した。追加の利上げが意識されるなか、ドル高主導の円安圧力が生じている。もっとも、
７月末頃に日米協調介入が実施されたことで、一方的な円安進行の可能性は低下した。当面は連続介入に対する
警戒感が残存しよう。
金利
日銀は10月に政策金利を1.25％で据え置いたが、先行きの利上げ方針は維持した。インフレ圧力が高まるなか、
円安に対する警戒もあり、３～４か月に一度の利上げが標準化する可能性が高まっている。長期金利はインフレ警
戒感が燻る下、２％台後半～３％台前半で推移するとみている。

Document 513: 世界経済見通し（日米欧亜・2026年11月）

Source PDF: all report pdf/世界経済見通し（日米欧亜・2026年11月）.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026年11月 17日

1/8 
世界経済見通し（日米欧亜・2026年 11月） 
 第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所 経済調査部

１．日本経済 
景気の現状 ～小幅マイナス成長も、景気回復の基調は維持～ 
2026年７-９月期の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は前期比年率▲0.1％と、４四半期ぶりのマイナス成長と
なった。個人消費が前期比▲0.3％と減少したほか、住宅投資や公共投資も減少し、成長率を押し
下げている。一方、設備投資は前期比＋1.6％と高い伸びとなり、景気を下支えした。 
今回減少した個人消費については、過度に悲観する必要はないだろう。４-６月期にみられた自
動車やエアコンなどの耐久財需要の押し上げが一巡したことに加え、７-９月期には台風や大雨、
長雨などの天候不順が相次いだことも下押し要因となっている。こうした一時的な要因が大き
く、個人消費の基調が悪化したと判断する必要はない。一方、設備投資が高い伸びとなったこと
は好材料だ。企業収益が高水準を維持するなか、人手不足に対応した省力化投資やデジタル・Ａ
Ｉ関連投資などへの需要は根強い。 
７-９月期はマイナス成長とはいえ、減少幅は僅かだ。前期まで３四半期連続で比較的高い成長
が続いていたことに加え、天候不順など一時的な消費下押し要因が影響したとみられることも踏
まえると、今回のマイナス成長をもって景気回復の流れが途切れたと判断する必要はないだろ
う。家計部門の弱さを企業部門が補うなか、日本経済は均してみれば緩やかな回復基調にあると
評価できる。

（出所）内閣府「国民経済計算」

### Page 2

2/8 
景気の先行き ～物価高が重荷も、企業部門が下支え～ 
26年度後半については、既往の資源高や原材料費、物流費などの上昇が時間差を伴って川下へ
波及し、景気の下押し要因となる。食料品や日用品、耐久財など幅広い品目への価格転嫁が進む
ことに加え、ガソリン補助金の段階的縮小も物価を押し上げる。冬場の電気・ガス料金補助は一
定の負担軽減につながるものの、物価上昇率の再加速によって実質賃金の改善が一服し、家計の
実質購買力が再び圧迫される可能性がある。企業にとっても原材料費や物流費などの上昇が収益
の重荷となるだろう。特に家計部門では、食料品など生活必需品の価格水準が高止まりしている
ことも、節約意識の強まりを通じて個人消費の回復を妨げる要因となる。 
もっとも、こうしたコスト上昇による下押しに対しては、高水準の企業収益が一定の緩衝材と
なる。ＡＩ関連需要の拡大も、半導体製造装置や電子部品などの輸出・生産、設備投資を押し上
げている。人手不足を背景とした省力化投資やデジタル関連投資への需要は根強く、海外景気の
底堅さも輸出を支えるだろう。設備投資は輸入資本財価格や建設コストの上昇による下押しを受
けながらも、増加基調を維持するとみられる。個人消費についても、高い賃上げや底堅い雇用環
境が支えとなり、物価高による下押しが強まるなかでも腰折れする可能性は低い。家計と企業の
回復に濃淡は残るものの、日本経済は緩やかな回復基調を維持するだろう。 
27年度は、原油高や原材料費上昇による物価上昇圧力が徐々に弱まり、景気の回復感が強まる
とみている。26年秋の原油高の影響は年度前半まで残るものの、年度後半には価格転嫁の動きも
次第に落ち着くだろう。物価上昇率の鈍化によって家計の実質購買力が改善し、高い賃上げもあ
いまって個人消費の回復力が強まると予想する。食料品への消費税減税と就業者負担軽減支援金
の支給も消費を押し上げる。企業部門では、コスト上昇圧力の緩和による収益環境の改善が設備
投資を支え、省力化やデジタル・ＡＩ関連を中心に増加が続くだろう。輸出も海外景気の底堅さ
やＡＩ関連需要に支えられる見込みである。家計、企業双方で持ち直しが進むことで、27年度の
実質ＧＤＰ成長率は＋1.2％と、26年度の＋0.9％から伸びを高めると予想する。

（シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト 新家 義貴 TEL 050-5474-7490）

（出所）米労働省より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成
２．米国経済
景気の現状 ～政策不確実性の下でもAI投資と個人消費が牽引し、底堅さを維持～
トランプ政権の「米国第一主義」に基づく貿易・安全保障政策の再構築が進み、関税強化や厳
格な不法移民取り締まりを通じて、国内外の地政学的・政策的不確実性は極めて高い水準にあ

る。特に中東情勢の緊迫化とホルムズ海峡の事実上の封鎖は、サプライチェーンの混乱や原油価
格の高騰・不安定化を招いた。しかし足元の米経済は、AI関連を中心とするIT投資の急速な拡大
が下支えとなり、強い底堅さを維持している。
7-9月期の実質GDP成長率（1次推計）は前期比年率＋3.4％と、4-6月期の同＋2.2％から伸びが
加速した（前年同期比は＋2.1％と 4-6月期の同＋2.2％からほぼ横ばい）。また、民間国内最終需
要（実質）が個人消費やAI投資の拡大によって前期比年率＋4.9％（4-6月期：同＋4.6％）へと伸
長しており、米国内需要の強さが改めて示された。
10-12月期も拡大基調は維持されている。10月のISM景気指数をみると、製造業が 55.5（前月：
54.5）、非製造業が 54.5（前月：54.9）とともに 50を大きく上回る高水準を維持している。
労働市場も底堅さを保っている。10月の非農業部門雇用者数は、前月差＋16.5万人（前月改定
値：同＋6.0万人）と増加幅が拡大した。移動平均で雇用の基調をみると、同雇用者数の6カ月移

動平均も同＋8.3万人（前月：同＋8.0万人）へ小幅に加速した。このように、単月のノイズや過
去分の改定、さらには高い政策不確実性や高金利環境の下でも、雇用拡大のモメンタムが継続し
ている。また、失業率が 4.1％という低い水準にあることなどを踏まえると、労働市場全体の安定
性は保たれているといえる。
物価面では、10月のコアCPIの上昇モメンタムが 3カ月前対比年率で＋3.7％（9月：＋3.4％）
と加速し、6カ月前対比年率でも＋2.7％（9月：＋2.9％）と高水準で推移している。特に短期的
トレンドを示す3カ月前対比年率がFRBのインフレ目標（2.0％）を大きく上回って加速している
ことは、ディスインフレ（物価減速）プロセスが足踏み状態にあることを示唆している。このデ
ータは、12月のFOMCにおいてFRBが追加利上げを検討・実施する強い根拠となる。なお、FRBは9
月のFOMCにおいてFFレート誘導目標レンジを 3.75～4.00％とし、3年 2カ月ぶりの利上げを決定
した後、10月のFOMCでは政策金利を据え置いた。
